# Cold-start acting-mode blast-radius audit — DEEP AUDIT (for codex review)

**Status**: DRAFT deep-audit (2026-05-23). Triggered by the OP-1619 acting-flip:
two cold-start over-reaches surfaced in shadow preview (Startup-1 tried to start
RETIRED units → fixed by OP-1621; Startup-2 would revert a human META → this audit).
Before filing more piecemeal fixes, this audits the FULL cold-start 4-phase blast
radius in acting mode. Code = shipped `~/sora-bridge/.../pipeline_coordinator.py`
(develop @ OP-1621).

## The systematic finding
**No cold-start phase honors any operator-intent guard.** The steady-state §6 path
filters on `coord-skip`/`coord-quarantine` (`coordinator_jql` :979) and the OP-1614
executor re-checks labels + live-runner. But the **cold-start boot recovery is a
separate, more aggressive path** whose phase queries select purely on runner-centric
staleness — they do NOT exclude `coord-skip`, `tier:X`, human-assigned, or META
tickets. So in acting mode, cold-start boot mutates operator/human tickets it cannot
distinguish from abandoned runner work.

## Per-phase mutation inventory (acting=True)
| Phase | selects | mutates | guard today |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Startup-1** infra (`_startup_1_infra` :2079) | audit `down` units | `start_unit` each; halt+`mention_operator` if still down | **OP-1621**: now only `expected=yes` (retired/gated excluded) ✓ |
| **Startup-2** reconcile (`_startup_2_reconcile` :2282; `interrupted_tickets` :1186) | **ALL `進行中`** tickets, `ORDER BY updated ASC` | (b) mergeable→`transition_under_review`; (a) commits→`mark_resumable`; (c) no-commits+stale→**`reset_to_todo`**; (d) recent→`mention_operator` | **NONE** — no coord-skip / tier:X / human / META filter |
| **Startup-3** sweep (`_build_stale_sweep_plan` :1602; `_startup_3_sweep`) | `claim:* OR runner-blocked:waiting-* OR (To Do AND assignee)` | drop `claim:*`; **`clear_assignee`** orphan; drop dead waiting markers | **NONE** — no coord-skip / tier:X / human filter |
| **L6** crash-recovery (`_l6_crash_recovery` :2118) | decision-log `shutdown_began` w/o `complete` + preceding real-action tick | re-issue relabel/transition; escalate file_ticket | only fires on a non-dry-run tick that crashed mid-drain — empty under shadow history |

Per-phase caps (OP-1618, default 1/1/1) bound the COUNT per boot, not WHICH ticket
or whether it is a legitimate target.

## Live blast radius (enumerated against prod JIRA, 2026-05-23)
**Startup-2 interrupted set (`status in In Progress/進行中`) = 2 tickets, BOTH over-reach:**
- `OP-1619` — tier:X, no assignee — **the acting-flip GATE ticket itself** (would be
  reconciled/mentioned). Not runner work.
- `OP-739` — HUMAN (nagita), tier:X, META (`priority:meta`), now also `coord-skip` —
  nagita's "CI parallel-gate program" tracking META. `reset_to_todo` when stale
  (its `updated` was 2026-05-14 — only transiently fresh because adding `coord-skip`
  bumped it; coord-skip is NOT honored by Startup-2, so it goes stale → reverts again).
- **Zero legitimate abandoned-runner tickets** in the set today → Startup-2 would
  currently mutate ONLY over-reach targets.

**Startup-3 sweep set (~50+):** overwhelmingly 公開済み/Archived tickets carrying a
stale `claim:default` + bot assignee → `claim:*` drop (harmless cleanup, but live
writes, 1/boot). Plus To-Do bot-assigned tickets (e.g. OP-231 claude-bot [HOLD]) →
`clear_assignee`. A **HUMAN-assigned To-Do** ticket would ALSO get its assignee
cleared (no human guard).

## Side effects (why this matters for the flip)
1. **Reverts human-owned in-progress work** (OP-739): a long-lived human/META ticket
   legitimately sits in 進行中; cold-start reads stale-`updated` as "abandoned runner
   work" → reverts to To Do, clearing context. Disrupts the human.
2. **Noises operator gate tickets** (OP-1619 tier:X): reconcile/mention on tickets
   that exist precisely as human gates.
3. **Clears human assignees** (Startup-3): an operator who self-assigns a To-Do
   ticket would have it silently cleared.
4. **coord-skip is a false promise at boot**: operators (and this very session) reach
   for `coord-skip` to protect a ticket, but cold-start ignores it — a latent
   foot-gun.
5. Caps (1/1/1) reduce volume but the FIRST-touched (oldest `updated`) is often the
   most-stale legit-looking human/META ticket.

## Recommended fix scope (for the ticket — pending codex)
Add operator-intent guards to the cold-start phase QUERIES so boot recovery matches
the steady-state path's respect for operator intent:
- **S1 (primary):** `interrupted_tickets` (Startup-2) + `_build_stale_sweep_plan`
  (Startup-3) JQL must EXCLUDE `coord-skip` and `coord-quarantine` (mirror
  `coordinator_jql` :979). Closes the false-promise (#4) + protects OP-739.
- **S2:** also exclude **`tier:X`** from both (tier:X is operator/human-gate by
  definition — never runner work; already excluded from runner pickup JQL). Closes
  OP-1619 + OP-739 (both tier:X).
- **S3 (consider):** skip **human-assigned** tickets in reconcile/sweep (assignee not
  a known bot account) — protects human in-progress work + human self-assignments
  regardless of tier. Needs a bot-account allowlist (claude-bot/codex-bot/...).
- **S4 (consider):** skip **META** (`priority:meta`/`type:meta`) tickets in Startup-2
  reconcile — a META legitimately sits 進行中 as a roll-up, never "abandoned runner
  work".

## Open questions for codex
- Q1: is S1+S2 (coord-skip + tier:X exclusion in both queries) sufficient, or are
  S3/S4 (human-assignee / META) needed for correctness (any over-reach S1+S2 misses)?
- Q2: bot-account allowlist for S3 — where is the canonical list (so "human" = not in
  it)? Is there drift risk (Family F per-instance bots)?
- Q3: should the guards live in the JQL (server-side filter) or in the Python
  classification (post-fetch)? JQL is cheaper + can't be forgotten by a caller; but
  `labels not in (...)` + `assignee` filters interact with the existing `ORDER BY`.
- Q4: does excluding tier:X from Startup-2/3 ever drop a LEGITIMATE recovery (is any
  real runner work ever tier:X)? (Runner pickup JQL excludes tier:X, so a tier:X
  ticket should never have been runner-claimed — confirm.)
- Q5: Startup-3 claim-drop on 公開済み/Archived tickets (50+) — harmless cleanup or
  should done-state tickets be excluded too (avoid 50 pointless writes over 50 boots)?
- Q6: anything else in the cold-start path (L6, the halt/mention) that mutates
  operator/human state and this audit missed.

## Relationship to the flip (OP-1619)
HARD pre-flip blocker (in addition to OP-1621, already merged). Until the cold-start
honors operator intent, flipping `acting=True` would mutate human/operator tickets on
boot. Fix ticket(s) to be filed AFTER this audit + codex review; OP-1619 blockedBy them.

  1168	        try:
  1169	            proc = subprocess.run(

  1177	            return False

  1181	                unit, proc.returncode, proc.stderr.strip(),
  1182	            )

  1187	        client = self._jira()

  1189	            return []
  1190	        try:

  1196	                "ORDER BY updated ASC"
  1197	            )

  1201	            )
  1202	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
  1203	            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] interrupted-ticket query failed: %s", exc)
  1204	            return []
  1205	        from backend.agents.pipeline_coordinator_rules import (
  1206	            DEFAULT_CLI_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,

  1209	
  1210	        stale_after = DEFAULT_CLI_TIMEOUT_SECONDS * STALE_MULTIPLIER
  1211	        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
  1212	        out: list[InterruptedTicket] = []
  1213	        for issue in resp.get("issues", []):
  1214	            fields = issue.get("fields") or {}
  1215	            status = str((fields.get("status") or {}).get("name", ""))
  1216	            # ``updated`` is a coarse last-activity proxy for staleness (the
  1217	            # exact 進行中-entry time needs the changelog; updated is a safe,
  1218	            # cheaper lower bound — if it's old, the ticket is certainly stale).
  1219	            stale = False
  1220	            updated_raw = fields.get("updated")
  1221	            if isinstance(updated_raw, str):
  1222	                try:
  1223	                    updated = datetime.fromisoformat(updated_raw.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
  1224	                    stale = (now - updated).total_seconds() > stale_after
  1225	                except ValueError:
  1226	                    pass
  1227	            out.append(
  1228	                InterruptedTicket(key=issue.get("key", "?"), status=status, stale=stale)
  1229	            )

  1297	            jira_dispatch.remove_label(client, key, label)
  1298	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
  1299	            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] remove_label(%s, %s) failed: %s", key, label, exc)

  1314	        client = self._jira()

  1316	            logger.error("[pipeline_coordinator] OPERATOR-ALERT (JIRA down) %s: %s", key, message)
  1317	            return

  1319	            from backend.agents import jira_dispatch
  1320

  1322	            if urgency == "high":
  1323	                jira_dispatch.add_label(client, key, "needs-operator-action")
  1324	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
  1325	            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] mention_operator(%s) failed: %s", key, exc)

  1339	class ShadowColdStartGateway:
  1340	    """Observe-only :class:`ColdStartGateway` for the 7-day shadow canary (OP-1555).
  1341	
  1342	    Wraps a real gateway and forwards every *read* (``audit_infra`` /
  1343	    ``interrupted_tickets`` / ``stale_sweep_plan`` + the per-ticket probes) so
  1344	    the 4-phase recovery still sees the true world — observation is the whole
  1345	    point of shadow mode — but turns every *mutator* into a record-only no-op:
  1580	        [
  1581	            "ssh", "-i", str(ssh_key), "-p", str(jira_dispatch.GERRIT_SSH_PORT),
  1582	            f"{user}@{jira_dispatch.GERRIT_SSH_HOST}",
  1583	            "gerrit", "query", "--format=JSON", f"is:open message:{key}",
  1584	        ],
  1585	        capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15,
  1586	    )
  1587	    if result.returncode != 0:

  1593	            continue

  1602	def _build_stale_sweep_plan(client: Any, *, repo_root: Path | None = None) -> StaleSweepPlan:
  1603	    """Assemble the Startup-3 sweep plan from a JIRA snapshot (ADR §3.3).
  1604	
  1605	    Three hygiene classes, each gated on liveness / staleness so a healthy
  1606	    in-flight ticket is never swept:
  1607	      - ``claim:default:*`` labels with no live runner → drop the claim
  1608	      - To-Do tickets bot-assigned with no ``claim:*`` → clear the assignee
  1609	      - ``runner-blocked:waiting-X`` where blocker X is 公開済み → drop marker
  1610	    """
  1611	    from backend.agents import jira_dispatch
  1612	    from backend.agents.pipeline_coordinator_rules import (
  1613	        CLAIM_LABEL_PREFIX,
  1614	        DEPENDENCY_WAITING_LABEL_PREFIX,
  1615	        PUBLISHED_STATUS_NAMES,
  1616	    )
  1617	
  1618	    repo_root = Path(repo_root) if repo_root is not None else _repo_root()
  1619	    jql = (
  1620	        f'project = "{client.project_key}" '
  1621	        'AND (labels ~ "claim:*" OR labels ~ "runner-blocked:waiting-*" '
  1622	        'OR (status = "To Do" AND assignee is not EMPTY)) '
  1623	        "ORDER BY updated ASC"
  1624	    )
  1625	    resp = jira_dispatch._request(
  1626	        client, "POST", "/search/jql",
  1627	        {"jql": jql, "fields": ["status", "labels", "assignee"], "maxResults": 200},
  1628	    )
  1629	    stale_claims: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {}
  1630	    orphan_assignees: list[str] = []
  1631	    resolved_waiting: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {}
  1632	    for issue in resp.get("issues", []):
  1633	        key = issue.get("key", "?")
  1634	        fields = issue.get("fields") or {}
  1635	        status = str((fields.get("status") or {}).get("name", ""))
  1636	        labels = list(fields.get("labels") or [])
  1637	        assignee = fields.get("assignee")
  1638	        claim_labels = tuple(l for l in labels if l.startswith(CLAIM_LABEL_PREFIX))
  1639	        if claim_labels and not _ticket_has_live_runner(key, repo_root=repo_root):
  1640	            stale_claims[key] = claim_labels
  1641	        elif assignee and status in jira_dispatch.TODO_STATUS_NAMES and not claim_labels:
  1642	            orphan_assignees.append(key)
  1643	        waiting = tuple(l for l in labels if l.startswith(DEPENDENCY_WAITING_LABEL_PREFIX))
  1644	        dead: list[str] = []
  1645	        for label in waiting:
  1646	            blocker_key = label[len(DEPENDENCY_WAITING_LABEL_PREFIX):]
  1647	            try:
  1648	                blocker_status = jira_dispatch.get_issue_status(client, blocker_key)
  1649	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
  1650	                continue
  1651	            if blocker_status in PUBLISHED_STATUS_NAMES:
  1652	                dead.append(label)
  1653	        if dead:
  1654	            resolved_waiting[key] = tuple(dead)
  1655	    return StaleSweepPlan(
  1656	        stale_claims=stale_claims,
  1657	        orphan_assignees=tuple(orphan_assignees),
  1658	        resolved_waiting=resolved_waiting,
  1659	    )

  1664	) -> ColdStartGateway:
  1665	    """Production gateway wiring (no I/O at construction — lazy + fail-open).

  2072	                "crash_recoveries": len(report.crash_recoveries),
  2073	                "reconciled": len(report.reconciled),

  2079	    def _startup_1_infra(self, report: ColdStartReport) -> None:
  2080	        """Startup-1: verify infra; start any down unit; halt if still down."""
  2081	        gw = self._cold_start_gateway
  2082	        audit = gw.audit_infra()
  2083	        starts_remaining = self._config.cold_start_max_infra
  2084	        for unit in audit.down:
  2085	            if starts_remaining <= 0:
  2086	                report.still_down_units.append(unit)
  2087	                self._log_cold_start(
  2088	                    {"event": STARTUP_PHASE_EVENT, "phase": 1, "unit": unit,
  2089	                     "action": "start_unit", "ok": False, "reason": "phase_cap"}
  2090	                )
  2091	                continue
  2092	            starts_remaining -= 1
  2093	            ok = gw.start_unit(unit)
  2094	            (report.started_units if ok else report.still_down_units).append(unit)
  2095	            self._log_cold_start(
  2096	                {"event": STARTUP_PHASE_EVENT, "phase": 1, "unit": unit,
  2097	                 "action": "start_unit", "ok": ok}
  2098	            )
  2099	        # Re-verify after starts: a unit that refused to start (or died again)
  2100	        # leaves the infra degraded → @-operator + halt (do not enter loop).
  2101	        if report.still_down_units:
  2102	            recheck = gw.audit_infra()
  2103	            report.still_down_units = list(recheck.down)
  2104	        if report.still_down_units:
  2105	            report.phase1_halted = True
  2106	            gw.mention_operator(
  2107	                "OP",  # infra-level alert is not ticket-scoped
  2108	                "[cold-start Startup-1] coordinator could not bring up "
  2109	                f"{report.still_down_units}; halting startup rather than "
  2110	                "entering the loop with degraded infra (ADR-0021 §3.3).",
  2111	                urgency="high",
  2112	            )
  2113	            self._log_cold_start(
  2114	                {"event": STARTUP_PHASE_EVENT, "phase": 1, "action": "halt",
  2115	                 "still_down": report.still_down_units}
  2116	            )
  2117	
  2118	    def _l6_crash_recovery(self, report: ColdStartReport) -> None:
  2119	        """L6 (ADR §9): replay last 24h decision log; resume/complete/rollback.
  2120	
  2121	        A ``tick_intent`` with no matching ``decision_tick`` (paired by
  2122	        ``decision_id``) means the daemon may have crashed after intent append
  2123	        and before outcome append. We re-run those planned real actions while
  2124	        the idempotency store is still inside its 24h TTL. The legacy
  2125	        ``shutdown_began`` path remains as a fallback for pre-intent drains.
  2126	        Every resolution writes a ``crash_recovery_applied`` record (ADR §9 L6).
  2127	        """
  2128	        records = self._read_recent_decision_log()
  2129	        began: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
  2130	        completed: set[str] = set()
  2131	        outcome_ticks: set[str] = set()
  2132	        intents: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
  2133	        last_real_action_tick: dict[str, Any] | None = None
  2134	        for rec in records:
  2135	            event = rec.get("event")
  2136	            if event == "tick_intent":
  2137	                decision_id = str(rec.get("decision_id", ""))
  2138	                if decision_id:
  2139	                    intents[decision_id] = rec
  2140	            elif event == "decision_tick":
  2141	                decision_id = str(rec.get("decision_id", ""))
  2142	                if decision_id:
  2143	                    outcome_ticks.add(decision_id)
  2144	                if not rec.get("dry_run", True) and rec.get("actions"):
  2145	                    last_real_action_tick = rec
  2146	            elif event == "shutdown_began":
  2147	                began[str(rec.get("decision_id", ""))] = rec
  2148	                # Snapshot which real-action tick preceded this drain.
  2149	                rec["_preceding_real_tick"] = last_real_action_tick
  2150	            elif event == "shutdown_complete":
  2151	                completed.add(str(rec.get("decision_id", "")))
  2152	        for decision_id, intent in intents.items():
  2153	            if decision_id in outcome_ticks:
  2154	                continue
  2155	            resolutions = self._resolve_interrupted_actions(intent)
  2156	            entry = {
  2157	                "event": CRASH_RECOVERY_EVENT,
  2158	                "crashed_decision_id": decision_id,
  2159	                "source_event": "tick_intent",
  2160	                "resolutions": resolutions,
  2161	            }
  2162	            report.crash_recoveries.append(entry)
  2163	            self._log_cold_start(entry)
  2164	        for drain_id, drain_rec in began.items():
  2165	            if drain_id in completed:
  2166	                continue  # clean shutdown — nothing to recover
  2167	            tick = drain_rec.get("_preceding_real_tick")
  2168	            resolutions = self._resolve_interrupted_actions(tick)
  2169	            entry = {
  2170	                "event": CRASH_RECOVERY_EVENT,
  2171	                "crashed_drain_id": drain_id,
  2172	                "resolutions": resolutions,
  2173	            }
  2174	            report.crash_recoveries.append(entry)
  2175	            self._log_cold_start(entry)
  2176	
  2177	    def _resolve_interrupted_actions(self, tick: dict[str, Any] | None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  2178	        """Map each interrupted action of a crashed tick to a recovery step."""
  2179	        if not tick:
  2180	            return [{"resolution": "noop", "reason": "no in-flight real action before crash"}]
  2181	        if tick.get("event") == "tick_intent":
  2182	            if tick.get("dry_run", True):
  2183	                return [{"resolution": "noop", "reason": "dry-run intent"}]
  2184	            if not self._within_l6_replay_window(tick):
  2185	                return [{"resolution": "skipped", "reason": "intent outside idempotency ttl"}]
  2186	            replayable: list[tuple[Action, list[str]]] = []
  2187	            for rec in tick.get("actions", []):
  2188	                action = _action_from_record(rec)
  2189	                if action is None:
  2190	                    continue
  2191	                keys = rec.get("idem_keys")
  2192	                replayable.append(
  2193	                    (
  2194	                        action,
  2195	                        [str(key) for key in keys] if isinstance(keys, list) else [],
  2196	                    )
  2197	                )
  2198	            actions = tuple(action for action, _keys in replayable)
  2199	            if not actions:
  2200	                return [{"resolution": "noop", "reason": "intent has no replayable actions"}]
  2201	            ctx = self.build_context()
  2202	            action_results = self._execute_tick_actions(actions, ctx)
  2203	            out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  2204	            for (action, intent_keys), result in zip(replayable, action_results, strict=False):
  2205	                out.append(
  2206	                    {
  2207	                        "kind": action.kind,
  2208	                        "target": action.target,
  2209	                        "resolution": "completed" if result.get("executed") else "replayed",
  2210	                        "idem_keys": intent_keys or _planned_action_idem_keys(action),
  2211	                        "action_result": result,
  2212	                    }
  2213	                )
  2214	            return out
  2215	        gw = self._cold_start_gateway
  2216	        out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  2217	        for action in tick.get("actions", []):
  2218	            kind = action.get("kind")
  2219	            target = action.get("target", "")
  2220	            if kind in (ACTION_RELABEL, ACTION_TRANSITION):
  2221	                # Idempotent → safe to complete (re-issue) on recovery.
  2222	                if kind == ACTION_TRANSITION and action.get("params", {}).get(
  2223	                    "to_status"
  2224	                ) == "Under Review":
  2225	                    gw.transition_under_review(target)
  2226	                resolution = "completed"
  2227	            elif kind == ACTION_FILE_TICKET:
  2228	                # Not safely reversible/repeatable — surface to operator.
  2229	                gw.mention_operator(
  2230	                    target,
  2231	                    f"[cold-start L6] crash interrupted a file_ticket action on {target}; "
  2232	                    "verify the dependency ticket exists and was not double-filed.",
  2233	                    urgency="medium",
  2234	                )
  2235	                resolution = "escalated"
  2236	            else:
  2237	                resolution = "noop"
  2238	            out.append({"kind": kind, "target": target, "resolution": resolution})
  2239	        return out
  2240	
  2241	    def _within_l6_replay_window(self, record: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bool:
  2242	        ts = record_ts(record)
  2243	        if ts is None:
  2244	            return True
  2245	        return ts >= self._clock() - timedelta(hours=L6_REPLAY_WINDOW_HOURS)
  2246	
  2247	    def _read_recent_decision_log(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  2248	        """Return decision-log records from the last :data:`L6_REPLAY_WINDOW_HOURS`.
  2249	
  2250	        Reads the day-partitioned JSONL files spanning the window (today + the
  2251	        days it reaches back into), in chronological order, skipping malformed
  2252	        lines so a single torn record never aborts recovery.
  2253	        """
  2254	        now = self._clock()
  2255	        window_start = now - timedelta(hours=L6_REPLAY_WINDOW_HOURS)
  2256	        records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  2257	        day = window_start.date()
  2258	        seen_paths: list[Path] = []
  2259	        while day <= now.date():
  2260	            path = self._decision_log.directory / f"{day.isoformat()}.jsonl"
  2261	            if path.exists():
  2262	                seen_paths.append(path)
  2263	            day = day + timedelta(days=1)
  2264	        for path in seen_paths:
  2265	            try:
  2266	                lines = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
  2267	            except OSError:
  2268	                continue
  2269	            for line in lines:
  2270	                if not line.strip():
  2271	                    continue
  2272	                try:
  2273	                    rec = json.loads(line)
  2274	                except json.JSONDecodeError:
  2275	                    continue
  2276	                if isinstance(rec, dict):
  2277	                    ts = record_ts(rec)
  2278	                    if ts is None or ts >= window_start:
  2279	                        records.append(rec)
  2280	        return records
  2281	
  2282	    def _startup_2_reconcile(self, report: ColdStartReport) -> None:
  2283	        """Startup-2: reconcile each interrupted (進行中) ticket (ADR §3.3).
  2284	
  2285	        Decision tree per ticket with no live runner:
  2286	          (b) Gerrit change exists + mergeable      → transition Under Review
  2287	          (a) feature branch has commits             → mark resumable, leave
  2288	          (c) no commits + stale (>2× CLI timeout)   → revert To Do (clear claim)
  2289	          (d) no commits + not yet stale (ambiguous) → @-operator, leave alone
  2290	        A ticket whose runner is still alive is left untouched (it's handling
  2291	        it). (b) is checked before (a): a mergeable change means the work is
  2292	        *finished* and only missed its transition, which beats re-running it.
  2293	        (c) vs (d) hinges on staleness so a runner that only just started (and
  2294	        hasn't committed yet) is never aggressively reverted out from under.
  2295	        """
  2296	        gw = self._cold_start_gateway
  2297	        mutations_remaining = self._config.cold_start_max_reconcile
  2298	        for ticket in gw.interrupted_tickets():
  2299	            key = ticket.key
  2300	            if gw.has_live_runner(key):
  2301	                self._record_reconcile(report, key, "live-runner", "leave")
  2302	            elif gw.gerrit_change_mergeable(key):
  2303	                if mutations_remaining <= 0:
  2304	                    self._record_reconcile(report, key, "gerrit-mergeable", "phase_cap")
  2305	                    continue
  2306	                mutations_remaining -= 1
  2307	                gw.transition_under_review(key)
  2308	                self._record_reconcile(report, key, "gerrit-mergeable", "under_review")
  2309	            elif gw.branch_has_commits(key):
  2310	                if mutations_remaining <= 0:
  2311	                    self._record_reconcile(report, key, "branch-has-commits", "phase_cap")
  2312	                    continue
  2313	                mutations_remaining -= 1
  2314	                gw.mark_resumable(key)
  2315	                self._record_reconcile(report, key, "branch-has-commits", "resume")
  2316	            elif ticket.stale:
  2317	                # No commits, no live runner, long-stale → abandoned work.
  2318	                if mutations_remaining <= 0:
  2319	                    self._record_reconcile(report, key, "no-commits-stale", "phase_cap")
  2320	                    continue
  2321	                mutations_remaining -= 1
  2322	                gw.reset_to_todo(key)
  2323	                self._record_reconcile(report, key, "no-commits-stale", "revert_todo")
  2324	            else:
  2325	                # No commits but recent — a runner may have only just started.
  2326	                if mutations_remaining <= 0:
  2327	                    self._record_reconcile(report, key, "ambiguous", "phase_cap")
  2328	                    continue
  2329	                mutations_remaining -= 1
  2330	                gw.mention_operator(
  2331	                    key,
  2332	                    "[cold-start Startup-2(d)] interrupted with no commits but "
  2333	                    "recent activity — ambiguous; leaving in place for operator review.",
  2334	                    urgency="medium",
  2335	                )
  2336	                self._record_reconcile(report, key, "ambiguous", "operator")
  2337	
  2338	    def _record_reconcile(
  2339	        self, report: ColdStartReport, key: str, classification: str, action: str
  2340	    ) -> None:
  2341	        entry = {"event": STARTUP_PHASE_EVENT, "phase": 2, "ticket": key,
  2342	                 "classification": classification, "action": action}
  2343	        report.reconciled.append({"ticket": key, "classification": classification, "action": action})
  2344	        self._log_cold_start(entry)
  2345	
  2346	    def _startup_3_sweep(self, report: ColdStartReport) -> None:
  2347	        """Startup-3: drop orphaned claim:* / dead waiting-* + clear assignees."""
  2348	        gw = self._cold_start_gateway
  2349	        plan = gw.stale_sweep_plan()
  2350	        mutations_remaining = self._config.cold_start_max_sweep
  2351	        for key, labels in plan.stale_claims.items():
  2352	            removed: list[str] = []
  2353	            for label in labels:
  2354	                if mutations_remaining <= 0:
  2355	                    self._record_sweep(report, key, "stale-claim", removed)

     1	"""Pipeline-coordinator decision-engine seam (29f-coord skeleton).
     2	
     3	ADR-0021 §2.1 specifies a *hybrid* decision engine: deterministic Python
     4	Tier-1 rules handle ≥90 % of decisions, with LLM consultation reserved for
     5	novel / multi-plausible cases. None of that logic exists yet — this is the
     6	foundation ticket (29f-coord). What ships here is the **contract** the real
     7	rule set (29f-3), cold-start orchestrator (29f-7) and event subscribers
     8	(29f-8) all plug into without changing the daemon:
     9	
    10	    DecisionContext  — everything the engine is allowed to read for a tick
    11	    DecisionEngine.evaluate(ctx) -> DecisionResult
    12	    Action / NoopAction          — the action vocabulary
    13	    DecisionResult               — actions + reason + decision_id
    14	
    15	The skeleton engine is intentionally empty: it returns a single
    16	``NoopAction`` with reason ``skeleton_noop`` and ``dry_run=True`` so the
    17	daemon can run end-to-end (heartbeat + decision-log) before any real
    18	decision logic exists.
    19	
    20	Module-global state audit (per project SOP)
    21	-------------------------------------------
    22	Frozen dataclasses, a Protocol, and a stateless engine class. No module
    23	globals, no import-time I/O. ``engine_version`` is a class constant so the
    24	decision-log records which engine produced each tick.
    25	"""
    26	
    27	from __future__ import annotations
    28	
    29	import inspect

    51	# grep-able in the decision log per ADR-0021 §9 L4).
    52	SKELETON_NOOP_REASON = "skeleton_noop"
    53	
    54	# ── Action vocabulary (ADR-0021 §6.1) ─────────────────────────────────
    55	# The *closed* set of action kinds a rule (or the Tier-2 LLM) may emit.
    56	# §6.1 deliberately keeps this list closed for safety; new kinds are added
    57	# by reviewable operator patch (§12 open-question 6), never by an LLM
    58	# proposal. Anything outside this set is dropped by the action layer.
    59	ACTION_NOOP = "noop"
    60	ACTION_FILE_TICKET = "file_ticket"
    61	ACTION_RELABEL = "relabel"
    62	ACTION_TRANSITION = "transition"
    63	ACTION_MENTION_OPERATOR = "mention_operator"
    64	ACTION_MARK_FOR_FOLLOWUP = "mark_for_followup"
    65	ACTION_ESCALATE = "escalate"
    66	
    67	ALLOWED_ACTION_KINDS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    68	    {
    69	        ACTION_NOOP,
    70	        ACTION_FILE_TICKET,
    71	        ACTION_RELABEL,
    72	        ACTION_TRANSITION,
    73	        ACTION_MENTION_OPERATOR,
    74	        ACTION_MARK_FOR_FOLLOWUP,
    75	        ACTION_ESCALATE,
    76	    }
    77	)
    78	
    79	
    80	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    81	class Action:
    82	    """A single thing the coordinator decides to do.
    83	
    84	    The skeleton only ever emits :class:`NoopAction`; the Tier-1 rule set
    85	    (29f-3) emits the §6.1 action kinds via the factory classmethods below.
    86	    The envelope is unchanged: ``kind`` is one of :data:`ALLOWED_ACTION_KINDS`,
    87	    ``target`` is the JIRA key the action applies to, ``params`` carries the
    88	    kind-specific payload, and ``dry_run`` defaults to True so a half-wired
    89	    (or shadow-mode, 29f-14) daemon can never mutate JIRA/Gerrit.
    90	
    91	    "Union type" (Code AC): :class:`Action` + :class:`NoopAction` over the
    92	    constrained :data:`ALLOWED_ACTION_KINDS` *is* the action union — a closed
    93	    set discriminated by ``kind`` rather than a ``typing.Union`` of N classes,
    94	    so the decision-log serialisation (:meth:`to_record`) stays uniform.
    95	    """
    96	
    97	    kind: str
    98	    target: str = ""
    99	    params: Mapping[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
   100	    dry_run: bool = True
   101	
   102	    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
   103	        if not isinstance(self.kind, str) or not self.kind.strip():
   104	            raise ValueError("Action.kind must be a non-empty string")
   105	        if self.kind not in ALLOWED_ACTION_KINDS:
   106	            raise ValueError(
   107	                f"Action.kind {self.kind!r} is not in the §6.1 allowed set "
   108	                f"{sorted(ALLOWED_ACTION_KINDS)}"
   109	            )
   110	        object.__setattr__(self, "params", dict(self.params))
   111	
   112	    def to_record(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   113	        """Serialisable form embedded in a decision-log ``actions`` array."""
   114	        return {
   115	            "kind": self.kind,
   116	            "target": self.target,
   117	            "params": dict(self.params),
   118	            "dry_run": self.dry_run,
   119	        }
   120	
   121	    # ── §6.1 factory constructors ─────────────────────────────────────
   122	    # Each rule emits one of these so payloads stay well-shaped (the action
   123	    # layer reads fixed param keys). ``dry_run`` defaults True everywhere;
   124	    # the action layer / shadow-mode toggle flips it, never a rule.
   125	
   126	    @classmethod
   127	    def file_ticket(
   128	        cls,
   129	        *,
   130	        blocking: str,
   131	        target_area: str,
   132	        description: str,
   133	        dry_run: bool = True,
   134	    ) -> "Action":
   135	        """§6.1 file_ticket — file a dependency/work ticket in another area."""
   136	        return cls(
   137	            kind=ACTION_FILE_TICKET,
   138	            target=blocking,
   139	            params={
   140	                "blocking": blocking,
   141	                "target_area": target_area,
   142	                "description": description,
   143	            },
   144	            dry_run=dry_run,
   145	        )
   146	
   147	    @classmethod
   148	    def relabel(
   149	        cls,
   150	        ticket: str,
   151	        *,
   152	        add: tuple[str, ...] = (),
   153	        remove: tuple[str, ...] = (),
   154	        dry_run: bool = True,
   155	    ) -> "Action":
   156	        """§6.1 relabel — add/remove labels (never operator-only labels)."""
   157	        return cls(
   158	            kind=ACTION_RELABEL,
   159	            target=ticket,
   160	            params={"add": list(add), "remove": list(remove)},
   161	            dry_run=dry_run,
   162	        )
   163	
   164	    @classmethod
   165	    def transition(
   166	        cls, ticket: str, *, to_status: str, dry_run: bool = True
   167	    ) -> "Action":
   168	        """§6.1 transition — move a ticket through an allowed status edge."""
   169	        return cls(
   170	            kind=ACTION_TRANSITION,
   171	            target=ticket,
   172	            params={"to_status": to_status},
   173	            dry_run=dry_run,
   174	        )
   175	
   176	    @classmethod
   177	    def mention_operator(
   178	        cls,
   179	        ticket: str,
   180	        *,
   181	        message: str,
   182	        urgency: str = "medium",
   183	        dry_run: bool = True,
   184	    ) -> "Action":
   185	        """§6.1 mention_operator — @-mention operator; high urgency tags too."""
   186	        return cls(
   187	            kind=ACTION_MENTION_OPERATOR,
   188	            target=ticket,
   189	            params={"message": message, "urgency": urgency},
   190	            dry_run=dry_run,
   191	        )
   192	
   193	    @classmethod
   194	    def mark_for_followup(
   195	        cls, ticket: str, *, when: str, why: str, dry_run: bool = True
   196	    ) -> "Action":
   197	        """§6.1 mark_for_followup — defer attention via coord-resume-after."""
   198	        return cls(
   199	            kind=ACTION_MARK_FOR_FOLLOWUP,
   200	            target=ticket,
   201	            params={"when": when, "why": why},
   202	            dry_run=dry_run,
   203	        )
   204	
   205	    @classmethod
   206	    def escalate(cls, ticket: str, *, reason: str, dry_run: bool = True) -> "Action":
   207	        """§6.1 escalate — needs-operator-action label + reason comment."""
   208	        return cls(
   209	            kind=ACTION_ESCALATE,
   210	            target=ticket,
   211	            params={"reason": reason},
   212	            dry_run=dry_run,
   213	        )
   214	
   215	
   216	@dataclass(frozen=True)
   217	class NoopAction(Action):
   218	    """The do-nothing action the skeleton engine returns every tick."""

     2	
     3	ADR-0021 §2.1 specifies a *hybrid* decision engine: deterministic Python
     4	Tier-1 rules handle ≥90 % of decisions, with LLM consultation reserved for
     5	novel / multi-plausible cases. None of that logic exists yet — this is the
     6	foundation ticket (29f-coord). What ships here is the **contract** the real
     7	rule set (29f-3), cold-start orchestrator (29f-7) and event subscribers
     8	(29f-8) all plug into without changing the daemon:
     9	
    10	    DecisionContext  — everything the engine is allowed to read for a tick
    11	    DecisionEngine.evaluate(ctx) -> DecisionResult

   224	@dataclass(frozen=True)
   225	class DecisionContext:
   226	    """Everything the engine may read to make one decision (a "tick").
   227	
   228	    Stateless-across-restarts (ADR-0021 §3.2): the daemon re-builds this
   229	    each tick from the injected clock + work-graph cache + capacity
   230	    snapshot. The skeleton populates only ``now`` and ``capacity``; later
   231	    phases add the JIRA work graph, bridge-log tail and event payload.
   232	    """
   233	
   234	    now: datetime
   235	    capacity: CapacitySnapshot
   236	    mode: str = "skeleton"
   237	    # Per-situation personality inputs (ADR-0021 §7). The daemon attaches a
   238	    # ``situation`` profile + any operator ``coord-mode:*`` override when a
   239	    # tick is actually deciding about a ticket/event; an idle tick leaves
   240	    # both ``None`` so the engine keeps the skeleton ``mode``.
   241	    situation: SituationProfile | None = None
   242	    mode_override: str | None = None
   243	    # Open-ended bag for later phases (event payload, work-graph cache,
   244	    # bridge-log tail). Skeleton leaves it empty.
   245	    work_graph: Mapping[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
   246	    # 29f-3: the ticket this tick is reasoning about (the "focal" ticket).
   247	    # ``None`` when the daemon ticks with an empty work-graph (skeleton /
   248	    # idle); the Tier-1 rules each early-return ``None`` in that case.
   249	    ticket: "Ticket | None" = None
   250	    # The wider work-graph slice the cross-ticket rules traverse
   251	    # (runner-blocked-marker-stale resolves the blocker's status;
   252	    # chain-deadlock walks the blockedBy DAG). Keyed by JIRA key.
   253	    tickets: Mapping[str, "Ticket"] = field(default_factory=dict)
   254	
   255	    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
   256	        if self.now.tzinfo is None:
   257	            raise ValueError("DecisionContext.now must be timezone-aware")
   258	        object.__setattr__(self, "work_graph", dict(self.work_graph))
   259	        object.__setattr__(self, "tickets", dict(self.tickets))
   260

  1340	    """Observe-only :class:`ColdStartGateway` for the 7-day shadow canary (OP-1555).
  1341	
  1342	    Wraps a real gateway and forwards every *read* (``audit_infra`` /
  1343	    ``interrupted_tickets`` / ``stale_sweep_plan`` + the per-ticket probes) so
  1344	    the 4-phase recovery still sees the true world — observation is the whole
  1345	    point of shadow mode — but turns every *mutator* into a record-only no-op:
  1346	    the would-be ``reset_to_todo`` / ``mark_resumable`` /
  1347	    ``transition_under_review`` / ``remove_label`` / ``clear_assignee`` /
  1348	    ``start_unit`` / ``mention_operator`` is logged (and lands in the decision
  1349	    log via the orchestrator's per-phase ``startup_phase`` records) without
  1350	    ever touching live JIRA / git / systemctl.
  1351	
  1352	    This is the cold-start sibling of :class:`ShadowActionExecutor` and of the
  1353	    ``sprint_replan(shadow=not acting)`` wiring: the single ``acting`` switch in
  1354	    :func:`build_default_coordinator` flips engine + sprint-replan + cold-start
  1355	    together. Before OP-1555, ``build_default_coordinator`` wired the live
  1356	    gateway unconditionally, so a coordinator deployed "in shadow mode"
  1357	    (``acting=False``) still mutated live JIRA on every boot — the gap this
  1358	    closes (sibling of OP-1552).
  1359	    """
  1360	
  1361	    #: Marks this gateway observe-only (parallels ``ShadowActionExecutor.executed``).
  1362	    acting: bool = False
  1363	
  1364	    def __init__(self, delegate: ColdStartGateway) -> None:
  1365	        self._delegate = delegate
  1366	
  1367	    # ── reads: forwarded unchanged (observation is the point) ──
  1368	    def audit_infra(self) -> InfraAuditResult:
  1369	        return self._delegate.audit_infra()
  1370	
  1371	    def interrupted_tickets(self) -> list[InterruptedTicket]:
  1372	        return self._delegate.interrupted_tickets()
  1373	
  1374	    def has_live_runner(self, key: str) -> bool:
  1375	        return self._delegate.has_live_runner(key)
  1376	
  1377	    def gerrit_change_mergeable(self, key: str) -> bool:
  1378	        return self._delegate.gerrit_change_mergeable(key)
  1379	
  1380	    def branch_has_commits(self, key: str) -> bool:
  1381	        return self._delegate.branch_has_commits(key)
  1382	
  1383	    def stale_sweep_plan(self) -> StaleSweepPlan:
  1384	        return self._delegate.stale_sweep_plan()
  1385	
  1386	    # ── mutators: record-only (never touch live JIRA / git / systemctl) ──
  1387	    def start_unit(self, unit: str) -> bool:
  1388	        # Record the would-be systemctl start and report success so the
  1389	        # observe-only boot proceeds past Startup-1 to actually observe the
  1390	        # reconcile + sweep phases, rather than halting on infra it was
  1391	        # explicitly told not to touch.
  1392	        self._record_shadow("start_unit", unit)
  1393	        return True
  1394	
  1395	    def mark_resumable(self, key: str) -> None:
  1396	        self._record_shadow("mark_resumable", key)
  1397	
  1398	    def transition_under_review(self, key: str) -> None:
  1399	        self._record_shadow("transition_under_review", key)
  1400	
  1401	    def reset_to_todo(self, key: str) -> None:
  1402	        self._record_shadow("reset_to_todo", key)
  1403	
  1404	    def remove_label(self, key: str, label: str) -> None:
  1405	        self._record_shadow("remove_label", f"{key} {label}")
  1406	
  1407	    def clear_assignee(self, key: str) -> None:
  1408	        self._record_shadow("clear_assignee", key)
  1409	
  1410	    def mention_operator(self, key: str, message: str, *, urgency: str = "high") -> None:
  1411	        # An @-operator mention posts a JIRA comment + label — a live mutation,
  1412	        # so it is gated too (the Deploy AC: no cold-start mutation path may
  1413	        # fire while acting=False). The intent is still captured in the log.
  1414	        self._record_shadow("mention_operator", f"{key} ({urgency})")
  1415	
  1416	    def _record_shadow(self, mutator: str, target: str) -> None:
  1417	        logger.info(
  1418	            "[pipeline_coordinator] cold-start SHADOW (acting=False): recording "
  1419	            "would-be %s(%s); not mutating live state.",
  1420	            mutator,
  1421	            target,
  1422	        )
  1423	
  1424	
  1425	def _repo_root() -> Path:
  1426	    """Repo root holding ``scripts/`` + git worktree (this file is backend/agents/*)."""
  1427	    return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

  1430	def run_deployment_audit(
  1431	    *, repo_root: Path | None = None, timeout_seconds: float = 60.0
  1432	) -> InfraAuditResult:
  1433	    """Run ``scripts/deployment-audit.sh`` (29a) and parse its unit verdicts.
  1434	
  1435	    The script's ``DEPLOYMENT_AUDIT_JSONL_LOG`` hook is used to get a
  1436	    machine-readable row dump (rather than scraping the human table); each
  1437	    ``systemd-unit`` / ``systemd-timer`` row maps to an :class:`InfraUnit`
  1438	    (``live = status == "OK"``). The script exits non-zero when an
  1439	    expected-live row is red — that's expected input here, not an error, so
  1440	    the return code is ignored and only the JSONL is read.
  1441	    """
  1442	    repo_root = Path(repo_root) if repo_root is not None else _repo_root()
  1443	    script = repo_root / "scripts" / "deployment-audit.sh"
  1444	    if not script.exists():
  1445	        logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] deployment-audit.sh not found at %s", script)
  1446	        return InfraAuditResult()
  1447	    import tempfile
  1448	
  1449	    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(
  1450	        prefix="coord-audit-", suffix=".jsonl", delete=False
  1451	    ) as tmp:
  1452	        jsonl_path = Path(tmp.name)
  1453	    try:
  1454	        env = dict(os.environ)
  1455	        env["DEPLOYMENT_AUDIT_JSONL_LOG"] = str(jsonl_path)
  1456	        try:
  1457	            subprocess.run(
  1458	                ["bash", str(script)],
  1459	                cwd=str(repo_root),
  1460	                env=env,
  1461	                capture_output=True,
  1462	                text=True,
  1463	                timeout=timeout_seconds,
  1464	            )
  1465	        except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as exc:
  1660	
  1661	
  1662	def _default_cold_start_gateway(
  1663	    config: CoordinatorConfig, *, acting: bool = False
  1664	) -> ColdStartGateway:
  1665	    """Production gateway wiring (no I/O at construction — lazy + fail-open).
  1666	
  1667	    ``acting=False`` (the default — the 29f-14 7-day shadow canary, OP-1555)
  1668	    wraps the live gateway in :class:`ShadowColdStartGateway` so cold-start
  1669	    observes the world but performs zero JIRA/git/systemctl mutation;
  1670	    ``acting=True`` returns the live gateway so the 4-phase recovery mutates as
  1671	    designed. The same single ``acting`` switch gates the engine action layer
  1672	    and the sprint re-plan handler (see :func:`build_default_coordinator`).
  1673	    """
  1674	    live = JiraDispatchColdStartGateway(agent_class=config.jira_agent_class)
  1675	    return live if acting else ShadowColdStartGateway(live)

  1678	# ── Daemon ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1679	
  1680	
  1681	class PipelineCoordinator:
  1682	    """Skeleton coordinator daemon: tick → evaluate → log no-op → heartbeat.
  1683	
  1684	    Collaborators are injected for deterministic tests; ``from_env`` /
  1685	    default construction wires the production defaults. The daemon holds
  1686	    no durable state of its own beyond an in-memory work-graph cache STUB
  1687	    that is rebuilt every tick (ADR-0021 §3.2 stateless-across-restarts).
  1688	    """
  1689	
  1690	    def __init__(
  1691	        self,
  1692	        config: CoordinatorConfig,
  1693	        *,
  1694	        engine: DecisionEngine | None = None,
  1695	        mode_selector: ModeSelector | None = None,
  1696	        clock: Clock = _utc_now,
  1697	        heartbeat: HeartbeatWriter | None = None,
  1698	        decision_log: DecisionLog | None = None,
  1699	        capacity_provider: Callable[[], CapacitySnapshot] | None = None,
  1700	        work_graph_provider: Callable[[], WorkGraph] | None = None,
  1701	        action_executor: ActionExecutor | None = None,
  1702	        cold_start_gateway: ColdStartGateway | None = None,
  1703	        sleep: SleepFn | None = None,
  1704	        bridge_tailer: BridgeEventTailer | None = None,
  1705	        jira_poller: JiraEventPoller | None = None,
  1706	        deduper: EventDeduper | None = None,
  1707	        sprint_replan_handler: SprintReplanHandler | None = None,
  1708	        learning_loop: "Any | None" = None,
  1709	        acting: bool = False,
  1710	    ) -> None:
  1711	        self._config = config
  1712	        self._acting = acting
  1713	        self._engine = engine or DecisionEngine()
  1714	        self._mode_selector = mode_selector or ModeSelector()
  1715	        self._clock = clock
  1716	        self._heartbeat = heartbeat or HeartbeatWriter(config.heartbeat_path, clock=clock)
  1717	        self._decision_log = decision_log or DecisionLog(config.decision_log_dir, clock=clock)
  1718	        self._capacity_provider = capacity_provider or self._default_capacity
  1719	        # 29f-3: how the daemon learns the per-tick world. Default yields an
  1720	        # empty WorkGraph (skeleton / idle); 29f-8 injects the JIRA-poll +
  1721	        # bridge-tap builder. Every rule abstains on an empty graph.
  1722	        self._work_graph_provider = work_graph_provider or self._default_work_graph
  1723	        # 29f-3: action layer. Default is shadow (observe-only) so a rule that
  1724	        # fires never mutates JIRA until the real §6 layer / acting mode lands.
  1725	        executor = action_executor or ShadowActionExecutor().execute
  1726	        self._action_executor: ActionExecutor = executor
  1727	        # 29f-6: event sources (ADR §4) — bridge JSONL tail + 60s JIRA poll +
  1728	        # hourly sweep, coalesced through a 5-min deduper. They feed the
  1729	        # per-tick raw events staged for the work-graph builder.
  1730	        self._bridge_tailer = bridge_tailer or BridgeEventTailer(
  1731	            config.bridge_events_path or (config.config_dir / "bridge-events.jsonl")
  1732	        )
  1733	        self._jira_poller = jira_poller or JiraEventPoller(
  1734	            agent_class=config.jira_agent_class,
  1735	            clock=clock,
  1736	            interval_seconds=config.jira_poll_interval_seconds,
  1737	        )
  1738	        self._deduper = deduper or EventDeduper(
  1739	            window_seconds=config.event_dedupe_seconds,
  1740	            clock=clock,
  1741	        )
  1742	        self._sprint_replan_handler = sprint_replan_handler
  1743	        self._last_sweep_at: datetime | None = self._clock()
  1744	        # 29f-7: cold-start recovery seam. Default wraps the production
  1745	        # jira_dispatch / git / systemctl adapters (lazy + fail-open) in the
  1746	        # observe-only ShadowColdStartGateway (acting=False default, OP-1555);
  1747	        # build_default_coordinator(acting=True) injects the live gateway, and
  1748	        # tests inject a fake so the 4-phase decision tree is verified in
  1749	        # isolation.
  1750	        self._cold_start_gateway: ColdStartGateway = (
  1751	            cold_start_gateway or _default_cold_start_gateway(config)
  1752	        )
  1753	        # 29f-11: learning loop (ADR §10). Default ``None`` keeps the skeleton /
  1754	        # test daemon inert; build_default_coordinator wires the Cognee
  1755	        # write-back + 24h outcome-check + weekly graduation handlers. When
  1756	        # present, every Tier-2 tick is written back within the tick ("within
  1757	        # 60s", Integration AC) and the daily/weekly schedules are gated here.
  1758	        self._learning_loop = learning_loop
  1759	        self._learning_seeded = False
  1760	        self._stop = threading.Event()
  1761	        # Interruptible sleep: default waits on the stop event so SIGTERM
  1762	        # breaks the loop within one poll instead of one tick.
  1763	        self._sleep: SleepFn = sleep or self._stop.wait
  1764	        # Current-tick world (rebuilt every tick — ADR §3.2 stateless).
  1765	        self._work_graph: WorkGraph = WorkGraph()
  1766	        # 29f-6: raw source events collected this tick, staged for the
  1767	        # events→WorkGraph builder (29f-8). Empty until the first refresh.
  1768	        self._pending_events: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  1769	        self._signals_installed = False
  1770	
  1771	    @property
  1772	    def config(self) -> CoordinatorConfig:
  1773	        return self._config
  1774	
  1775	    # ── world-building (stateless per tick) ──
  2000	            record["tier"] = result.tier
  2001	        # 29f-6: the Tier-2 LLM-consultation block (ADR Appendix C) — only on a
  2002	        # tick that actually consulted (or degraded) the LLM, so a Tier-1 / no-op
  2003	        # tick keeps its minimal record shape and ``budget_cap`` events stay
  2004	        # grep-able + replayable for the daily-spend rebuild.
  2005	        if result.llm_consultation is not None:
  2006	            record["llm_consultation"] = dict(result.llm_consultation)
  2007	        if action_results:
  2008	            record["action_results"] = action_results
  2009	        return record
  2010	
  2011	    def _build_tick_intent_record(self, result: DecisionResult) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
  2012	        actions = [a for a in result.actions if not isinstance(a, NoopAction)]
  2013	        if not actions:
  2014	            return None
  2015	        action_records = []
  2016	        for action in actions:
  2017	            rec = action.to_record()
  2018	            rec["idem_keys"] = _planned_action_idem_keys(action)
  2019	            action_records.append(rec)
  2020	        return {
  2021	            "ts": self._clock().isoformat(),
  2022	            "event": "tick_intent",
  2023	            "engine_version": result.engine_version,
  2024	            "mode": result.mode,
  2025	            "decision_id": result.decision_id,
  2026	            "actions": action_records,
  2027	            "reason": result.reason,
  2028	            "dry_run": all(a.dry_run for a in actions),
  2029	            "pid": os.getpid(),
  2030	        }
  2031	
  2032	    # ── cold-start 4-phase recovery (ADR §3.3 + §9 L6) ──
  2033	
  2034	    def _log_cold_start(self, record: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
  2035	        """Append a cold-start decision-log record, stamping ts/pid/engine."""
  2036	        base: dict[str, Any] = {
  2037	            "ts": self._clock().isoformat(),
  2038	            "engine_version": self._engine.engine_version,
  2039	            "pid": os.getpid(),
  2040	        }
  2041	        base.update(record)
  2042	        self._decision_log.append(base)
  2043	
  2044	    def startup(self) -> ColdStartReport:
  2045	        """Run the 4-phase cold-start recovery (ADR §3.3). Returns a report.
  2046	
  2047	        Order: Startup-1 (infra) → L6 crash-recovery (ADR §9 L6: "after Phase
  2048	        Startup-1") → Startup-2 (JIRA reconcile) → Startup-3 (stale sweep) →
  2049	        Startup-4 (log steady-state entry). If Startup-1 cannot bring the
  2050	        expected infra up, the daemon halts startup (``phase1_halted=True``)
  2051	        and does NOT proceed — entering the loop with degraded infra is worse
  2052	        than not running (ADR §3.3 Startup-1 failure path). :meth:`run` reads
  2053	        the report to decide whether to enter the loop.
  2054	        """
  2055	        report = ColdStartReport()
  2056	        self._log_cold_start({"event": COLD_START_BEGAN_EVENT})
  2057	        self._startup_1_infra(report)
  2058	        if report.phase1_halted:
  2059	            self._log_cold_start(
  2060	                {"event": COLD_START_COMPLETE_EVENT, "halted_at": "startup-1",
  2061	                 "still_down": report.still_down_units}
  2062	            )
  2063	            return report
  2064	        self._l6_crash_recovery(report)
  2065	        self._startup_2_reconcile(report)
  2066	        self._startup_3_sweep(report)
  2067	        self._startup_4_enter_loop(report)
  2068	        self._log_cold_start(
  2069	            {
  2070	                "event": COLD_START_COMPLETE_EVENT,
  2071	                "started_units": report.started_units,
  2072	                "crash_recoveries": len(report.crash_recoveries),
  2073	                "reconciled": len(report.reconciled),
  2074	                "swept": len(report.swept),
  2075	            }
  2076	        )
  2077	        return report
  2078	
  2346	    def _startup_3_sweep(self, report: ColdStartReport) -> None:
  2347	        """Startup-3: drop orphaned claim:* / dead waiting-* + clear assignees."""
  2348	        gw = self._cold_start_gateway
  2349	        plan = gw.stale_sweep_plan()
  2350	        mutations_remaining = self._config.cold_start_max_sweep
  2351	        for key, labels in plan.stale_claims.items():
  2352	            removed: list[str] = []
  2353	            for label in labels:
  2354	                if mutations_remaining <= 0:
  2355	                    self._record_sweep(report, key, "stale-claim", removed)
  2356	                    return
  2357	                mutations_remaining -= 1
  2358	                gw.remove_label(key, label)
  2359	                removed.append(label)
  2360	            if mutations_remaining <= 0:
  2361	                self._record_sweep(report, key, "stale-claim", removed)
  2362	                return
  2363	            mutations_remaining -= 1
  2364	            gw.clear_assignee(key)
  2365	            self._record_sweep(report, key, "stale-claim", removed)
  2366	        for key in plan.orphan_assignees:
  2367	            if mutations_remaining <= 0:
  2368	                self._record_sweep(report, key, "orphan-assignee", [])
  2369	                return
  2370	            mutations_remaining -= 1
  2371	            gw.clear_assignee(key)
  2372	            self._record_sweep(report, key, "orphan-assignee", [])
  2373	        for key, labels in plan.resolved_waiting.items():
  2374	            removed: list[str] = []
  2375	            for label in labels:
  2376	                if mutations_remaining <= 0:
  2377	                    self._record_sweep(report, key, "resolved-waiting", removed)
  2378	                    return
  2379	                mutations_remaining -= 1
  2380	                gw.remove_label(key, label)
  2381	                removed.append(label)
  2382	            self._record_sweep(report, key, "resolved-waiting", removed)
  2383	
  2384	    def _record_sweep(
  2385	        self, report: ColdStartReport, key: str, kind: str, labels: list[str]
  2386	    ) -> None:
  2387	        entry = {"event": STARTUP_PHASE_EVENT, "phase": 3, "ticket": key,
  2388	                 "sweep": kind, "labels": labels}
  2389	        report.swept.append({"ticket": key, "sweep": kind, "labels": labels})
  2390	        self._log_cold_start(entry)
  2391	
  2392	    def _startup_4_enter_loop(self, report: ColdStartReport) -> None:
  2393	        """Startup-4: log steady-state entry (the loop itself is run() → run_forever)."""
  2394	        report.entered_loop = True
  2395	        self._log_cold_start(
  2396	            {"event": STEADY_STATE_ENTERED_EVENT, "ts_entered": self._clock().isoformat()}
  2397	        )
  2398	        logger.info(
  2399	            "[pipeline_coordinator] cold-start complete — entered steady state at %s",
  2400	            self._clock().isoformat(),
  2401	        )
  2402	
  2403	    def run(
  2404	        self,
  2405	        *,
  2406	        install_signals: bool = True,
  2407	        max_ticks: int | None = None,
  2408	    ) -> int:
  2409	        """Production boot: cold-start recovery, then the steady-state loop.
  2410	
  2411	        Runs :meth:`startup` first (ADR §3.3 — the coordinator is the canonical
  2412	        startup orchestrator). If Startup-1 halted on degraded infra, the loop
  2413	        is NOT entered and 0 ticks are returned. Otherwise hands off to
  2414	        :meth:`run_forever` (Startup-4).
  2415	        """
  2416	        report = self.startup()
  2417	        if report.phase1_halted:
  2418	            logger.error(
  2419	                "[pipeline_coordinator] cold-start halted in Startup-1 "
  2420	                "(infra still down: %s) — not entering steady-state loop",
  2421	                report.still_down_units,
  2422	            )
  2423	            return 0
  2424	        return self.run_forever(install_signals=install_signals, max_ticks=max_ticks)
  2425	
  2426	    # ── entrypoints ──
  2427	
  2428	    def run_once(self) -> DecisionResult:
  2429	        """One tick: heartbeat → refresh world → evaluate → log decision.
  2430	
  2431	        Returns the engine's :class:`DecisionResult` (a no-op in the
  2432	        skeleton) so callers/tests can assert on it directly.
  2433	        """
  2434	        self._heartbeat.touch()
  2435	        self._refresh_work_graph()
  2436	        self._append_source_event_records()
  2437	        ctx = self.build_context()
  2438	        if self._config.capacity_path is not None:
  2439	            write_capacity_snapshot(ctx.capacity, self._config.capacity_path)
  2440	        result = self._engine.evaluate(ctx)
  2441	        kill_switch = _env_flag(ACTING_KILL_ENV)
  2442	        if self._acting and not kill_switch:
  2443	            actions = tuple(
  2444	                a if isinstance(a, NoopAction) else dataclasses.replace(a, dry_run=False)
  2445	                for a in result.actions
  2446	            )
  2447	            result = dataclasses.replace(result, actions=actions)
  2448	        elif kill_switch:
  2449	            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] acting kill-switch active; observing only")
  2450	            actions = tuple(
  2451	                a if isinstance(a, NoopAction) else dataclasses.replace(a, dry_run=True)
  2452	                for a in result.actions
  2453	            )
  2454	            result = dataclasses.replace(result, actions=actions)
  2455	        fair_actions = self._fair_action_order(result.actions)
  2456	        if fair_actions is not result.actions:
  2457	            result = dataclasses.replace(result, actions=fair_actions)
  2458	        intent_record = self._build_tick_intent_record(result)
  2459	        if intent_record is not None:
  2460	            self._decision_log.append(intent_record)
  2461	        # Hand each real (non-noop) action to the action layer. In shadow
  2462	        # mode it only records the would-be execution; the outcomes go into
  2463	        # the decision-log line so a fired rule is observable end-to-end.
  2464	        action_results = self._execute_tick_actions(result.actions, ctx)
  2465	        record = self._build_tick_record(result, action_results)
  2466	        self._decision_log.append(record)
  2467	        self._run_learning_loop(record, result)
  2468	        return result
  2469	
  2470	    def _execute_tick_actions(
  2471	        self,
  2472	        actions: tuple[Action, ...],
  2473	        ctx: DecisionContext,
  2474	    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  2475	        max_actions = _env_int(
  2476	            MAX_ACTIONS_PER_TICK_ENV,
  2477	            DEFAULT_MAX_ACTIONS_PER_TICK,
  2478	            minimum=0,
  2479	        )
  2480	        attempted = 0
  2481	        action_results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  2482	        for action in actions:
  2483	            if isinstance(action, NoopAction):
  2484	                continue
  2485	            if attempted >= max_actions:
  2486	                logger.warning(
  2487	                    "[pipeline_coordinator] tick action cap reached for %s %s",
  2488	                    action.kind,
  2489	                    action.target,
  2490	                )
  2491	                action_results.append(
  2492	                    {
  2493	                        "kind": action.kind,
  2494	                        "target": action.target,
  2495	                        "executed": False,
  2496	                        "reason": "tick_cap",
  2497	                    }
  2498	                )
  2499	                continue
  2500	            attempted += 1
  2501	            try:
  2502	                action_results.append(self._action_executor(action, ctx))
  2503	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
  2504	                # A single bad executor call must not suppress the tick log.
  2505	                logger.warning(
  2506	                    "[pipeline_coordinator] action executor failed for %s %s: %s",
  2507	                    action.kind,
  2508	                    action.target,
  2509	                    exc,
  2510	                )
  2511	                action_results.append(
  2512	                    {
  2513	                        "kind": action.kind,
  2514	                        "target": action.target,
  2515	                        "executed": False,
  2516	                        "error": str(exc),
  2517	                    }
  2518	                )
  2519	        return action_results
  2520	
  2521	    def _run_learning_loop(self, record: dict[str, Any], result: DecisionResult) -> None:
  2522	        """Write back a Tier-2 decision + fire any due daily/weekly handler.
  2523	
  2524	        Gated on a wired learning loop (29f-11) so the skeleton / test daemon is
  2525	        unaffected. Write-back happens in-tick so a Tier-2 decision reaches
  2526	        Cognee well within the 60s Integration-AC budget; the daily + weekly
  2527	        schedules are gated by the loop's own cadence (mirrors the hourly
  2528	        sweep). Fail-open: a learning-loop error never breaks a decision tick.
  2529	        """
  2530	        loop = self._learning_loop
  2531	        if loop is None:
  2532	            return
  2533	        try:
  2534	            if not self._learning_seeded:
  2535	                loop.seed_schedule(self._clock())

/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/sop/architecture-anti-patterns.md:376:3. **File-set lock at planning time**: each child ticket declares its `Files / Paths` section explicitly. CI / pre-pickup gate refuses to start E2 if E1's open PS already declares the same file paths. This is OP-731 (file-mutex pickup gate) — shipped at the JIRA-sibling layer. **OP-800 extends the same gate to the runtime layer** (`backend/agents/jira_dispatch.py::file_mutex_check`): every pickup attempt also queries `_open_bot_owned_file_owners()` and refuses to start a candidate whose declared paths overlap a currently-open Gerrit PS, regardless of the sibling ticket's JIRA status. The skip comment cites the blocker's Gerrit change number, links back here, and lists the three resolution paths (merge / rebase / re-scope). Operator escape hatch: label `runner-mutex-override:file-overlap` on the candidate ticket bypasses the gate when the operator has an explicit hand-merge plan.

/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family5-image-surfacing-contract.md:563:### §8.2 Handoff to Family ⑩ (runner pickup)
/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family5-image-surfacing-contract.md:565:The runner (`auto-runner-codex.py` / `auto-runner-jira.py` / `auto-runner-multi.py`) reads `/version` to log which image version it is running under, recorded in the per-pickup audit row (per `v2-⑩-1bc`'s `runner_claims.external_refs` field). Implementing tickets in Family ⑩ MUST add `/version` read to the pickup logging path; v2-⑤-1bc's implementer should coordinate with the Family ⑩ implementer on the JSON shape.

/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/adr/ADR-0035-runner-fsm-and-error-handling.md:212:- **ADR-0037 Runner State Substrate Decoupling** (2026-05-14, OP-1105): supersedes the implicit label-based coordination substrate that ADR-0035 assumed but never named. The FSM, invariants I1–I6, §C4 Gerrit-first ordering, and §7 idempotency primitives remain binding; ADR-0037 narrows their *mechanism* so claim ownership, mutex hold, pickup progress, and terminal cleanup move from JIRA labels (`claim:*`, `runner-blocked:*`, `runner-loop-paused-pending-review`) to a Postgres `runner_claims` table. JIRA labels remain the human-facing ticket ledger; the runner's coordination authority moves to Postgres. See ADR-0037 §D4 for the per-label disposition table and §D3 for the shadow → enforce → drop migration plan.

/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/sprint-s12/sprint-s12g-A-v2-runtime-defense-contract-spec.md:253:**Filing-time materialization rule (v1.4, codex round-2 P2-6)**: every `required_area_labels:` entry inside a boundary block MUST be materialized as a JIRA label at filing time — they are NOT only stored as YAML metadata. The filing hook (extension of OP-1042) reads `required_area_labels` from each ticket's boundary block and rejects filing if the JIRA payload's `labels` field doesn't contain every entry. This closes the SOP §2 path-to-area rule loop: the YAML expresses intent, the JIRA payload carries the runtime label, the filing hook enforces consistency. Same rule applies to `required_paths:` — those don't materialize as labels but must be added to the ticket's description AC section so codex/claude pickup can verify "did I only touch files in the allowed set".

/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/sop/lessons/L-OP-977-idempotent-label-add-is-not-a-mutex.md:15:**Fix (OP-977 / AUDIT-24)**: replace the bare label with a **fencing token** — `claim:{instance_id}:{epoch_us:016d}-{uuid8}`, unique per attempt — and decide the winner by `min(...)` over *all* `claim:{instance_id}:*` labels in the post-PUT readback. The winner is now a function of a **total order over a set every racer observes identically**, not of "did my idempotent write succeed". The loser leaves its label for GC so the winner re-evaluating `min(...)` never flips. Stale tokens (crashed runners) are swept once aged past `2× CLI timeout`; pre-AUDIT-24 bare labels are treated as expired. Rollback flag `OMNISIGHT_RUNNER_ATOMIC_CLAIM_LEGACY=1`. Race-simulation suite `backend/tests/test_atomic_claim_race.py` reproduces the OP-974 schedule (two threads, same `instance_id`, same bot, fully interleaved GET/PUT/GET) and asserts exactly one wins and the lowest token wins for every token assignment. Pattern doc: `docs/sop/runner-pickup-mutex.md`.

/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/sop/jira-ticket-conventions.md:342:- `mutex_with`: list of `mutex:<resource-id>` strings. Runner pre-pickup gate (§16) hard-blocks pickup if any sibling ticket sharing one of these labels is currently `In Progress` or `Under Review`. Pickup is released as soon as the holder transitions out of those two states (Approved / Published / Archived / TODO all count as "released"). Implementation: `backend/agents/jira_dispatch.find_mutex_holders` runs `project = OP AND status in ("In Progress", "Under Review") AND (labels = … OR …) AND key != self`; non-empty result → blocked. The blocked candidate is skipped and the runner tries the next-best candidate from the dispatch queue. See OP-687 (operator concern 2026-05-07: overnight unattended runs producing silent merge collisions).

/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/runner-self-audit-launch.log:7863:If I (codex) had agency over runner design, the single most important first change would be to move claim ownership, runner-owned artifacts, pickup progress, and terminal cleanup out of JIRA labels/comments and into a small Postgres-backed coordination record, while leaving JIRA as the human-facing ticket ledger. The evidence is not abstract: on 2026-05-14 I repeatedly completed OP-1070's implementation work, then lost the pickup to runner-runtime artifacts ("'`.runner-cwd-sentinel`, `progress.txt`), stale claim residue, TOCTOU assignee divergence, and pre-sync failures. Those are not code-generation failures; they are coordination-state failures masquerading as task failures.
/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/runner-self-audit-launch.log:7869:From inside a pickup, the worst failure is: I implement the ticket, add tests, post AC verification, and then the runner reverts because its own files made the worktree look dirty or tampered. OP-1070 is the clearest case. Comments show codex picked it up at 2026-05-14 02:01:52, completed AC verification at 02:07:39, then reverted at 02:07:51 because `.runner-cwd-sentinel` had disappeared. Later I completed AC at 02:46:29 and reverted at 02:46:42 because the first uncommitted path was `progress.txt`. The same pattern hit claude on OP-1069 and OP-1071.
/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/runner-self-audit-launch.log:7879:When I pick up a ticket I do not get one authoritative lease. I get JIRA status, assignee, class label, claim labels, comments, worktree branch state, and sometimes bridge state. On OP-1070, after several reverted attempts, comments show repeated `[runner-presync-fail]` from `git switch -C feature/OP-1070-runner-fresh ...` in the codex-2 worktree, followed by fresh pickups from another codex worktree. Then at 02:55 and 03:14 the runner aborted because the assignee diverged from pickup (`live=None`). That is a textbook symptom of state-authority ambiguity: the runner is trying to infer whether it still owns the task from mutable JIRA fields that other automation also mutates.
/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/runner-self-audit-launch.log:7887:3. Health gates that are meant to protect me can block every pickup because they depend on the thing they are checking.
/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/runner-self-audit-launch.log:7889:OP-1067 added bridge-health as a runner pickup signal. OP-1077 then documents the follow-up: the bridge heartbeat was emitted from `_maintenance_ticks()` inside a loop blocked on `gerrit stream-events`, so quiet Gerrit periods made the heartbeat stale even when the process was alive. The result was that runner pickup would see `bridge_down` and block all work. The workaround was an external `while true; touch heartbeat; sleep 60` loop. I personally saw OP-1077 fail as codex: at 03:56:59 I picked it up, at 03:57:03 pre-sync failed, and later at 04:03:46 my completed verification was blocked by `runner-capability-blocked` because `gerrit_push` was not permitted.
/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/runner-self-audit-launch.log:7891:SP-B-X coverage: OP-1067 is the first-class bridge health gate; OP-1077 fixes the heartbeat cadence by moving heartbeat emission into a background thread. That is net-positive once deployed, but the first version introduced a fleet-wide pickup blocker.
/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/runner-self-audit-launch.log:7893:Operator intent: do not let agents push work if the Gerrit->JIRA bridge is down, because merged work could fail to publish ticket state. Architecture failure: the health signal was coupled to the bridge'"'s event loop and then used as a hard pickup gate. A protection mechanism became a single-point denial of service.
/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/runner-self-audit-launch.log:7895:What would have prevented it: health signals need independent liveness contracts and graded behavior. For a stale bridge, the runner can still pick up code-only work but hold finalization or mark \"review pending bridge\"; it should not blindly block all pickup unless the ticket requires bridge-dependent state mutation.

/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/runner-self-audit-and-redesign-2026-05-14.txt:3:If I (codex) had agency over runner design, the single most important first change would be to move claim ownership, runner-owned artifacts, pickup progress, and terminal cleanup out of JIRA labels/comments and into a small Postgres-backed coordination record, while leaving JIRA as the human-facing ticket ledger. /home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/runner-self-audit-and-redesign-2026-05-14.txt:9:From inside a pickup, the worst failure is: I implement the ticket, add tests, post AC verification, and then the runner reverts because its own files made the worktree look dirty or tampered. /home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/runner-self-audit-and-redesign-2026-05-14.txt:19:When I pick up a ticket I do not get one authoritative lease. /home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/runner-self-audit-and-redesign-2026-05-14.txt:27:3. Health gates that are meant to protect me can block every pickup because they depend on the thing they are checking.
/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/runner-self-audit-and-redesign-2026-05-14.txt:29:OP-1067 added bridge-health as a runner pickup signal. OP-1077 then documents the follow-up: the bridge heartbeat was emitted from `_maintenance_ticks()` inside a loop blocked on `gerrit stream-events`, so quiet Gerrit periods made the heartbeat stale even when the process was alive. The result was that runner pickup would see `bridge_down` and block all work. The workaround was an external `while true; touch heartbeat; sleep 60` loop. I personally saw OP-1077 fail as codex: at 03:56:59 I picked it up, at 03:57:03 pre-sync failed, and later at 04:03:46 my completed verification was blocked by `runner-capability-blocked` because `gerrit_push` was not permitted.

/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/runner-self-audit-and-redesign-2026-05-14.txt:33:Operator intent: do not let agents push work if the Gerrit->JIRA bridge is down, because merged work could fail to publish ticket state. /home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/runner-self-audit-and-redesign-2026-05-14.txt:35:What would have prevented it: health signals need independent liveness contracts and graded behavior. /home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/op-1608-sora-bridge-repoint-codex-audit-2026-05-22.txt:52:- **R1 — heartbeat gap on bridge restart → runners abstain.** Bridge writes heartbeat each cycle; a restart leaves a gap until the new process writes its first one. Runner stale-threshold default 60s (`heartbeat_stale_after_seconds`).
