/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/g-a-v2-v1.3-codex-launch.log:1104:If I (codex) had agency over runner design, the single most important first change would be to move claim ownership, runner-owned artifacts, pickup progress, and terminal cleanup out of JIRA labels/comments and into a small Postgres-backed coordination record, while leaving JIRA as the human-facing ticket ledger. Time horizon: 2-3 weeks for a strangler first pass that preserves current JIRA transitions. Confidence: high. The evidence is not abstract: on 2026-05-14 I repeatedly completed OP-1070's implementation work, then lost the pickup to runner-runtime artifacts (`.runner-cwd-sentinel`, `progress.txt`), stale claim residue, TOCTOU assignee divergence, and pre-sync failures. Those are not code-generation failures; they are coordination-state failures masquerading as task failures.
/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/g-a-v2-v1.3-codex-launch.log:1110:From inside a pickup, the worst failure is: I implement the ticket, add tests, post AC verification, and then the runner reverts because its own files made the worktree look dirty or tampered. OP-1070 is the clearest case. Comments show codex picked it up at 2026-05-14 02:01:52, completed AC verification at 02:07:39, then reverted at 02:07:51 because `.runner-cwd-sentinel` had disappeared. Later I completed AC at 02:46:29 and reverted at 02:46:42 because the first uncommitted path was `progress.txt`. The same pattern hit claude on OP-1069 and OP-1071.
/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/g-a-v2-v1.3-codex-launch.log:1120:When I pick up a ticket I do not get one authoritative lease. I get JIRA status, assignee, class label, claim labels, comments, worktree branch state, and sometimes bridge state. On OP-1070, after several reverted attempts, comments show repeated `[runner-presync-fail]` from `git switch -C feature/OP-1070-runner-fresh ...` in the codex-2 worktree, followed by fresh pickups from another codex worktree. Then at 02:55 and 03:14 the runner aborted because the assignee diverged from pickup (`live=None`). That is a textbook symptom of state-authority ambiguity: the runner is trying to infer whether it still owns the task from mutable JIRA fields that other automation also mutates.
/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/g-a-v2-v1.3-codex-launch.log:1128:3. Health gates that are meant to protect me can block every pickup because they depend on the thing they are checking.
/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/g-a-v2-v1.3-codex-launch.log:1130:OP-1067 added bridge-health as a runner pickup signal. OP-1077 then documents the follow-up: the bridge heartbeat was emitted from `_maintenance_ticks()` inside a loop blocked on `gerrit stream-events`, so quiet Gerrit periods made the heartbeat stale even when the process was alive. The result was that runner pickup would see `bridge_down` and block all work. The workaround was an external `while true; touch heartbeat; sleep 60` loop. I personally saw OP-1077 fail as codex: at 03:56:59 I picked it up, at 03:57:03 pre-sync failed, and later at 04:03:46 my completed verification was blocked by `runner-capability-blocked` because `gerrit_push` was not permitted.
/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/g-a-v2-v1.3-codex-launch.log:1132:SP-B-X coverage: OP-1067 is the first-class bridge health gate; OP-1077 fixes the heartbeat cadence by moving heartbeat emission into a background thread. That is net-positive once deployed, but the first version introduced a fleet-wide pickup blocker.
/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/g-a-v2-v1.3-codex-launch.log:1134:Operator intent: do not let agents push work if the Gerrit->JIRA bridge is down, because merged work could fail to publish ticket state. Architecture failure: the health signal was coupled to the bridge's event loop and then used as a hard pickup gate. A protection mechanism became a single-point denial of service.
/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/g-a-v2-v1.3-codex-launch.log:1136:What would have prevented it: health signals need independent liveness contracts and graded behavior. For a stale bridge, the runner can still pick up code-only work but hold finalization or mark "review pending bridge"; it should not blindly block all pickup unless the ticket requires bridge-dependent state mutation.
/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/audit/codex-reviews/g-a-v2-v1.3-codex-launch.log:1160:- In `jira_dispatch.pre_pickup_ok()`, keep `live_state_requires` and JIRA dependency checks, but replace `find_mutex_holders()` JQL label search with a coordination-table query.

   395	def _canonical_action_semantic_params(value: Any) -> Any:
   396	    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
   397	        return {
   398	            str(k): _canonical_action_semantic_params(v)
   399	            for k, v in sorted(value.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]))
   400	            if str(k) not in _VOLATILE_ACTION_PARAM_KEYS
   401	        }
   402	    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple, set, frozenset)):
   403	        normalised = [_canonical_action_semantic_params(v) for v in value]
   404	        if all(isinstance(v, (str, int, float, bool, type(None))) for v in normalised):
   405	            return sorted(normalised, key=lambda item: str(item))
   406	        return normalised
   407	    return value

   410	def _action_idem_base(
   411	    action: Action,
   412	    *,
   413	    semantic_params: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
   414	) -> str:
   415	    payload = {
   416	        "params": _canonical_action_semantic_params(
   417	            semantic_params if semantic_params is not None else action.params
   418	        )
   419	    }
   420	    material = json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), default=str)
   421	    digest = hashlib.sha256(material.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:16]
   422	    return f"coord:{action.kind}:{action.target}:{digest}"
   423

   425	def _file_ticket_operator_message(action: Action) -> str:
   426	    target_area = str(action.params.get("target_area") or "backend")
   427	    blocking = str(action.params.get("blocking") or action.target)
   428	    description = str(action.params.get("description") or "")
   429	    return (
   430	        "[coord-acting] file_ticket requires operator action: "
   431	        f"target_area={target_area}; blocking={blocking}; {description}"
   432	    )

   435	def _planned_action_idem_keys(action: Action) -> list[str]:
   436	    base_key = _action_idem_base(action)
   437	    if action.kind == ACTION_RELABEL:
   438	        keys = [f"{base_key}:add:{label}" for label in action.params.get("add", ())]
   439	        keys.extend(f"{base_key}:remove:{label}" for label in action.params.get("remove", ()))
   440	        removes_claim = any(
   441	            str(label).startswith(CLAIM_LABEL_PREFIX)
   442	            for label in action.params.get("remove", ())
   443	        )
   444	        if removes_claim:
   445	            keys.append(f"{base_key}:clear-assignee")
   446	        return keys
   447	    if action.kind == ACTION_TRANSITION:
   448	        return [f"{base_key}:transition"]
   449	    if action.kind == ACTION_MENTION_OPERATOR:
   450	        keys = [f"{base_key}:comment"]
   451	        if str(action.params.get("urgency") or "medium") == "high":
   452	            keys.append(f"{base_key}:label")
   453	        return keys
   454	    if action.kind == ACTION_MARK_FOR_FOLLOWUP:
   455	        return [f"{base_key}:label", f"{base_key}:comment"]
   456	    if action.kind == ACTION_ESCALATE:
   457	        return [f"{base_key}:label", f"{base_key}:comment"]
   458	    if action.kind == ACTION_FILE_TICKET:
   459	        semantic = dict(action.params)
   460	        semantic["operator_message"] = _file_ticket_operator_message(action)
   461	        file_base_key = _action_idem_base(action, semantic_params=semantic)
   462	        return [f"{file_base_key}:comment", f"{file_base_key}:label"]
   463	    return []

   467	    kind = record.get("kind")
   468	    if not isinstance(kind, str) or kind == ACTION_NOOP:
   469	        return None
   470	    target = str(record.get("target") or "")

   491	                "kind": action.kind,
   492	                "target": action.target,
   493	                "executed": False,
   494	                "reason": "noop",
   495	            }

   531	    def _jira(self) -> Any:
   532	        if self._client is None:
   533	            from backend.agents import jira_dispatch
   534	
   535	            self._client = jira_dispatch.make_client(self._config.jira_agent_class)
   536	        return self._client

   538	    def _pre_mutation_recheck(self, action: Action) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   539	        from backend.agents import jira_dispatch
   540	
   541	        try:
   542	            labels = set(jira_dispatch.fetch_ticket_labels(self._jira(), action.target))
   543	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   544	            logger.warning(
   545	                "[pipeline_coordinator] live label recheck failed for %s: %s",
   545	                "[pipeline_coordinator] live label recheck failed for %s: %s",
   546	                action.target,
   547	                exc,
   548	            )
   549	            return {
   550	                "kind": action.kind,
   551	                "target": action.target,
   552	                "executed": False,
   553	                "reason": "label_read_failed",
   554	            }
   555	        if "coord-skip" in labels or "coord-quarantine" in labels:
   556	            return {
   557	                "kind": action.kind,
   558	                "target": action.target,
   559	                "executed": False,
   560	                "reason": "coord_skip",
   561	            }
   562	        return None

   564	    def _destructive_transition_recheck(self, action: Action) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   565	        from backend.agents import jira_dispatch
   566	
   567	        try:
   568	            status = jira_dispatch.get_issue_status(self._jira(), action.target)
   569	            runner_live = _ticket_has_live_runner(action.target, fail_on_probe_error=True)
   570	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   571	            logger.warning(
   572	                "[pipeline_coordinator] destructive transition recheck failed for %s: %s",
   573	                action.target,
   574	                exc,
   575	            )
   576	            return {
   577	                "kind": action.kind,
   578	                "target": action.target,
   579	                "executed": False,
   580	                "reason": "context_stale",
   581	            }
   582	        if status not in jira_dispatch.IN_PROGRESS_STATUS_NAMES or runner_live:
   583	            return {
   584	                "kind": action.kind,
   585	                "target": action.target,
   586	                "executed": False,
   587	                "reason": "context_stale",
   588	            }
   589	        return None
   590	
   591	    def _execute_transition(self, action: Action) -> dict[str, Any]:
   592	        from backend.agents import jira_dispatch
   593	
   594	        target_status = str(action.params.get("to_status") or "")
   595	        base_key = _action_idem_base(action)
   596	        if target_status in jira_dispatch.TODO_STATUS_NAMES or target_status.lower() in {
   597	            "todo",
   598	            "to do",
   599	        }:
   600	            blocked = self._pre_mutation_recheck(action)
   601	            if blocked is not None:
   602	                return blocked
   603	            blocked = self._destructive_transition_recheck(action)
   604	            if blocked is not None:
   605	                return blocked
   606	            reason = str(action.params.get("reason") or "Coordinator live action requested To Do.")
   607	            jira_dispatch.transition_back_to_todo(
   608	                self._jira(),
   609	                action.target,
   610	                reason=reason,
   611	                idem_key=f"{base_key}:transition",
   612	            )
   613	            outcome = {"to_status": "To Do"}
   614	        elif target_status in jira_dispatch.UNDER_REVIEW_STATUS_NAMES or target_status.lower() in {
   615	            "under_review",
   616	            "under review",
   617	        }:
   618	            blocked = self._pre_mutation_recheck(action)
   619	            if blocked is not None:
   620	                return blocked
   621	            issued = jira_dispatch.transition_to_under_review_if_needed(
   622	                self._jira(),
   623	                action.target,
   624	                idem_key=f"{base_key}:transition",
   625	            )
   626	            outcome = {"to_status": "Under Review", "issued": issued}
   627	        else:
   628	            return {
   629	                "kind": action.kind,
   630	                "target": action.target,
   631	                "executed": False,
   632	                "reason": "unsupported_transition",
   633	                "outcome": {"to_status": target_status},
   634	            }
   635	        return {
   636	            "kind": action.kind,
   637	            "target": action.target,
   638	            "executed": True,
   639	            "outcome": outcome,
   640	        }
   641	
   642	    def _execute_relabel(self, action: Action) -> dict[str, Any]:
   643	        from backend.agents import jira_dispatch
   644	
   645	        blocked = self._pre_mutation_recheck(action)
   646	        if blocked is not None:
   647	            return blocked
   648	        add = tuple(str(x) for x in action.params.get("add", ()))
   649	        remove = tuple(str(x) for x in action.params.get("remove", ()))
   650	        base_key = _action_idem_base(action)
   651	        outcomes: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   652	        for label in add:
   653	            jira_dispatch.add_label(
   654	                self._jira(), action.target, label, idem_key=f"{base_key}:add:{label}"
   655	            )
   656	            outcomes.append({"op": "add", "label": label})
   657	        for label in remove:
   658	            jira_dispatch.remove_label(
   659	                self._jira(), action.target, label, idem_key=f"{base_key}:remove:{label}"
   660	            )
   661	            outcomes.append({"op": "remove", "label": label})
   662	            if label.startswith(CLAIM_LABEL_PREFIX):
   663	                jira_dispatch.clear_assignee(
   664	                    self._jira(), action.target, idem_key=f"{base_key}:clear-assignee"
   665	                )
   666	                outcomes.append({"op": "clear_assignee"})
   667	        return {
   668	            "kind": action.kind,
   669	            "target": action.target,
   670	            "executed": bool(outcomes),
   671	            "outcome": outcomes,
   672	        }
   673	
   674	    def _execute_mention(self, action: Action) -> dict[str, Any]:
   675	        from backend.agents import jira_dispatch
   676	
   677	        blocked = self._pre_mutation_recheck(action)
   678	        if blocked is not None:
   679	            return blocked
   680	        message = str(action.params.get("message") or "")
   681	        urgency = str(action.params.get("urgency") or "medium")
   682	        base_key = _action_idem_base(action)
   683	        jira_dispatch.add_comment(
   684	            self._jira(), action.target, message, idem_key=f"{base_key}:comment"
   685	        )
   686	        outcome: list[dict[str, Any]] = [{"op": "comment", "urgency": urgency}]
   687	        if urgency == "high":
   688	            jira_dispatch.add_label(
   689	                self._jira(),
   690	                action.target,
   690	                action.target,
   691	                NEEDS_OPERATOR_ACTION_LABEL,
   692	                idem_key=f"{base_key}:label",
   693	            )
   694	            outcome.append({"op": "label", "label": NEEDS_OPERATOR_ACTION_LABEL})
   695	        return {
   696	            "kind": action.kind,
   697	            "target": action.target,
   698	            "executed": True,
   699	            "outcome": outcome,
   700	        }
   701	
   702	    def _execute_followup(self, action: Action) -> dict[str, Any]:
   703	        from backend.agents import jira_dispatch
   704	
   705	        blocked = self._pre_mutation_recheck(action)
   706	        if blocked is not None:
   707	            return blocked
   708	        when = str(action.params.get("when") or "")
   709	        why = str(action.params.get("why") or "")
   710	        label = f"{FOLLOWUP_LABEL_PREFIX}{when}"
   711	        base_key = _action_idem_base(action)
   712	        jira_dispatch.add_label(
   713	            self._jira(), action.target, label, idem_key=f"{base_key}:label"
   714	        )
   715	        jira_dispatch.add_comment(
   716	            self._jira(), action.target, why, idem_key=f"{base_key}:comment"
   717	        )
   718	        return {
   719	            "kind": action.kind,
   720	            "target": action.target,
   721	            "executed": True,
   722	            "outcome": [{"op": "label", "label": label}, {"op": "comment"}],
   723	        }
   724	
   725	    def _execute_escalate(self, action: Action) -> dict[str, Any]:
   726	        from backend.agents import jira_dispatch
   727	
   728	        blocked = self._pre_mutation_recheck(action)
   729	        if blocked is not None:
   730	            return blocked
   731	        reason = str(action.params.get("reason") or "")
   732	        base_key = _action_idem_base(action)
   733	        jira_dispatch.add_label(
   734	            self._jira(),
   735	            action.target,
   736	            NEEDS_OPERATOR_ACTION_LABEL,
   737	            idem_key=f"{base_key}:label",
   738	        )
   739	        jira_dispatch.add_comment(
   740	            self._jira(), action.target, reason, idem_key=f"{base_key}:comment"
   741	        )
   742	        return {
   743	            "kind": action.kind,
   744	            "target": action.target,
   745	            "executed": True,
   746	            "outcome": [
   747	                {"op": "label", "label": NEEDS_OPERATOR_ACTION_LABEL},
   748	                {"op": "comment"},
   749	            ],
   750	        }
   751	
   752	    def _execute_file_ticket(self, action: Action) -> dict[str, Any]:
   753	        from backend.agents import jira_dispatch
   754	
   755	        blocked = self._pre_mutation_recheck(action)
   756	        if blocked is not None:
   757	            return blocked
   758	        message = _file_ticket_operator_message(action)
   759	        semantic = dict(action.params)
   760	        semantic["operator_message"] = message
   761	        base_key = _action_idem_base(action, semantic_params=semantic)
   762	        jira_dispatch.add_comment(
   763	            self._jira(), action.target, message, idem_key=f"{base_key}:comment"
   764	        )
   765	        jira_dispatch.add_label(
   766	            self._jira(),
   767	            action.target,
   768	            NEEDS_OPERATOR_ACTION_LABEL,
   769	            idem_key=f"{base_key}:label",
   770	        )
   771	        return {
   772	            "kind": action.kind,
   773	            "target": action.target,
   774	            "executed": False,
   775	            "reason": "requires_operator",
   776	            "outcome": [
   777	                {
   778	                    "kind": action.kind,
   779	                    "target": action.target,
   780	                    "executed": False,
   781	                    "reason": "requires_operator",
   782	                },
   783	                {
   784	                    "kind": ACTION_MENTION_OPERATOR,
   785	                    "target": action.target,
   786	                    "executed": True,
   787	                    "outcome": [
   788	                        {"op": "comment"},
   789	                        {"op": "label", "label": NEEDS_OPERATOR_ACTION_LABEL},
   790	                    ],
   791	                },
   792	            ],
   793	        }
   794	
   795	    def _idem_base(
   796	        self,
   797	        action: Action,
   798	        *,
   799	        semantic_params: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
   800	    ) -> str:
   801	        return _action_idem_base(action, semantic_params=semantic_params)
   802	
   803	    def _canonical_semantic_params(self, value: Any) -> Any:
   804	        return _canonical_action_semantic_params(value)

   707	@rule(name="stale-claim-cleanup", priority=40)
   708	def stale_claim_cleanup(ctx: DecisionContext) -> "Action | None":
   709	    """§5.1 #2 — ``claim:default:*`` > 2× CLI timeout, no live runner: clear.
   710	
   711	    Removes the orphaned claim label(s) (ADR §3.3 Startup-3 stale-state sweep
   712	    for a single ticket). Assignee clearing rides along in the action layer's
   713	    relabel handler. Abstains while a runner is alive or the claim is fresh.
   714	    """
   715	    t = ctx.ticket
   716	    if t is None or t.has_live_runner:
   717	        return None
   718	    claim_labels = labels_with_prefix(t, CLAIM_LABEL_PREFIX)
   719	    if not claim_labels:
   720	        return None
   721	    age = _age_seconds(ctx.now, t.claim_started_at)
   722	    if age is None or age <= _stale_threshold_seconds():
   723	        return None
   724	    return Action.relabel(t.key, remove=claim_labels)
   725	
   726	
   727	@rule(name="runner-blocked-marker-stale", priority=50)
   728	def runner_blocked_marker_stale(ctx: DecisionContext) -> "Action | None":
   729	    """§5.1 #3 — ``runner-blocked:waiting-X`` where X is 公開済み: drop marker.
   730	
   731	    Resolves each ``runner-blocked:waiting-<KEY>`` against ``ctx.tickets``;
   732	    any blocker already Published is a dead wait — remove that marker so the
   733	    blocked ticket is pickable again. Abstains if blockers are unknown or
   734	    still open.
   735	    """
   736	    t = ctx.ticket
   737	    if t is None:
   738	        return None
   739	    waiting = labels_with_prefix(t, DEPENDENCY_WAITING_LABEL_PREFIX)
   740	    stale: list[str] = []
   741	    for label in waiting:
   742	        blocker_key = label[len(DEPENDENCY_WAITING_LABEL_PREFIX):]
   743	        blocker = ctx.tickets.get(blocker_key)
   744	        if blocker is not None and blocker.status in PUBLISHED_STATUS_NAMES:
   745	            stale.append(label)
   746	    if not stale:
   747	        return None
   748	    return Action.relabel(t.key, remove=tuple(stale))
   749

  2282	    def _startup_2_reconcile(self, report: ColdStartReport) -> None:
  2283	        """Startup-2: reconcile each interrupted (進行中) ticket (ADR §3.3).
  2284	
  2285	        Decision tree per ticket with no live runner:
  2286	          (b) Gerrit change exists + mergeable      → transition Under Review
  2287	          (a) feature branch has commits             → mark resumable, leave
  2288	          (c) no commits + stale (>2× CLI timeout)   → revert To Do (clear claim)
  2289	          (d) no commits + not yet stale (ambiguous) → @-operator, leave alone
  2290	        A ticket whose runner is still alive is left untouched (it's handling
  2291	        it). (b) is checked before (a): a mergeable change means the work is
  2292	        *finished* and only missed its transition, which beats re-running it.
  2293	        (c) vs (d) hinges on staleness so a runner that only just started (and
  2294	        hasn't committed yet) is never aggressively reverted out from under.
  2295	        """
  2296	        gw = self._cold_start_gateway
  2297	        mutations_remaining = self._config.cold_start_max_reconcile
  2298	        for ticket in gw.interrupted_tickets():
  2299	            key = ticket.key
  2300	            if gw.has_live_runner(key):
  2301	                self._record_reconcile(report, key, "live-runner", "leave")
  2302	            elif gw.gerrit_change_mergeable(key):
  2303	                if mutations_remaining <= 0:
  2304	                    self._record_reconcile(report, key, "gerrit-mergeable", "phase_cap")
  2305	                    continue
  2306	                mutations_remaining -= 1
  2307	                gw.transition_under_review(key)
  2308	                self._record_reconcile(report, key, "gerrit-mergeable", "under_review")
  2309	            elif gw.branch_has_commits(key):
  2310	                if mutations_remaining <= 0:
  2311	                    self._record_reconcile(report, key, "branch-has-commits", "phase_cap")
  2312	                    continue
  2313	                mutations_remaining -= 1
  2314	                gw.mark_resumable(key)
  2315	                self._record_reconcile(report, key, "branch-has-commits", "resume")
  2316	            elif ticket.stale:
  2317	                # No commits, no live runner, long-stale → abandoned work.
  2318	                if mutations_remaining <= 0:
  2319	                    self._record_reconcile(report, key, "no-commits-stale", "phase_cap")
  2320	                    continue
  2321	                mutations_remaining -= 1
  2322	                gw.reset_to_todo(key)
  2323	                self._record_reconcile(report, key, "no-commits-stale", "revert_todo")
  2324	            else:
  2325	                # No commits but recent — a runner may have only just started.
  2326	                if mutations_remaining <= 0:
  2327	                    self._record_reconcile(report, key, "ambiguous", "phase_cap")
  2328	                    continue
  2329	                mutations_remaining -= 1
  2330	                gw.mention_operator(
  2331	                    key,
  2332	                    "[cold-start Startup-2(d)] interrupted with no commits but "
  2333	                    "recent activity — ambiguous; leaving in place for operator review.",
  2334	                    urgency="medium",
  2335	                )
  2336	                self._record_reconcile(report, key, "ambiguous", "operator")
  2337	
  2346	    def _startup_3_sweep(self, report: ColdStartReport) -> None:
  2347	        """Startup-3: drop orphaned claim:* / dead waiting-* + clear assignees."""
  2348	        gw = self._cold_start_gateway
  2349	        plan = gw.stale_sweep_plan()
  2350	        mutations_remaining = self._config.cold_start_max_sweep
  2351	        for key, labels in plan.stale_claims.items():
  2352	            removed: list[str] = []
  2353	            for label in labels:
  2354	                if mutations_remaining <= 0:
  2355	                    self._record_sweep(report, key, "stale-claim", removed)
  2356	                    return
  2357	                mutations_remaining -= 1
  2358	                gw.remove_label(key, label)
  2359	                removed.append(label)
  2360	            if mutations_remaining <= 0:
  2361	                self._record_sweep(report, key, "stale-claim", removed)
  2362	                return
  2363	            mutations_remaining -= 1
  2364	            gw.clear_assignee(key)

  2367	            if mutations_remaining <= 0:
  2368	                self._record_sweep(report, key, "orphan-assignee", [])
  2369	                return
  2370	            mutations_remaining -= 1
  2371	            gw.clear_assignee(key)
  2372	            self._record_sweep(report, key, "orphan-assignee", [])
  2373	        for key, labels in plan.resolved_waiting.items():
  2374	            removed: list[str] = []
  2375	            for label in labels:
  2376	                if mutations_remaining <= 0:
  2377	                    self._record_sweep(report, key, "resolved-waiting", removed)
  2378	                    return
  2379	                mutations_remaining -= 1
  2380	                gw.remove_label(key, label)
  2381	                removed.append(label)
  2382	            self._record_sweep(report, key, "resolved-waiting", removed)

   478	
   479	class LiveActionExecutor:
   480	    """Execute ADR-0021 §6 actions against JIRA with stable idempotency keys."""
   481	
   482	    executed: bool = True
   483	
   484	    def __init__(self, config: CoordinatorConfig, *, client: Any | None = None) -> None:
   485	        self._config = config
   486	        self._client = client
   487	
   488	    def execute(self, action: Action, ctx: DecisionContext) -> dict[str, Any]:
   489	        if action.kind == ACTION_NOOP:
   490	            return {
   491	                "kind": action.kind,
   492	                "target": action.target,
   493	                "executed": False,
   494	                "reason": "noop",
   495	            }
   496	        if action.kind not in {
   497	            ACTION_RELABEL,
   498	            ACTION_TRANSITION,
   499	            ACTION_MENTION_OPERATOR,
   500	            ACTION_MARK_FOR_FOLLOWUP,
   501	            ACTION_ESCALATE,
   502	            ACTION_FILE_TICKET,
   503	        }:
   504	            return {
   505	                "kind": getattr(action, "kind", "unknown"),
   506	                "target": getattr(action, "target", ""),
   507	                "executed": False,
   508	                "reason": "unsupported",
   509	            }
   510	        if action.dry_run:
   511	            return {
   512	                "kind": action.kind,
   513	                "target": action.target,
   514	                "executed": False,
   515	                "reason": "dry_run",
   516	            }
   517	        if action.kind == ACTION_RELABEL:
   518	            return self._execute_relabel(action)
   519	        if action.kind == ACTION_TRANSITION:
   520	            return self._execute_transition(action)
   521	        if action.kind == ACTION_MENTION_OPERATOR:
   522	            return self._execute_mention(action)
   523	        if action.kind == ACTION_MARK_FOR_FOLLOWUP:
   524	            return self._execute_followup(action)
   525	        if action.kind == ACTION_ESCALATE:
   526	            return self._execute_escalate(action)
   527	        if action.kind == ACTION_FILE_TICKET:
   528	            return self._execute_file_ticket(action)
   529	        raise AssertionError(f"unhandled action kind: {action.kind}")
   530	
   531	    def _jira(self) -> Any:
   532	        if self._client is None:
   533	            from backend.agents import jira_dispatch
   534	
   535	            self._client = jira_dispatch.make_client(self._config.jira_agent_class)
  2535	                loop.seed_schedule(self._clock())
  2536	                self._learning_seeded = True
  2537	            if result.tier == 2:
  2538	                loop.write_back_decision(record)
  2539	            loop.maybe_run(self._clock())
  2540	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — learning loop is best-effort
  2541	            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] learning loop error: %s", exc)
  2542	
  2543	    def _append_source_event_records(self) -> None:
  2544	        for event in self._pending_events:
  2545	            self._decision_log.append(
  2546	                {
  2547	                    "ts": self._clock().isoformat(),
  2548	                    "event": "coordinator_source_event",
  2549	                    "trigger": event.get("trigger"),
  2550	                    "source": event.get("source"),
  2551	                    "ticket_key": event.get("ticket_key"),
  2552	                    "payload": event.get("payload"),
  2553	                    "dry_run": True,
  2554	                    "pid": os.getpid(),
  2555	                }
  2556	            )
  2557	
  2558	    def request_stop(self) -> None:
  2559	        """Ask the steady-state loop to stop after the current tick."""
  2560	        self._stop.set()
  2561	
  2562	    @property
  2563	    def stopping(self) -> bool:
  2564	        return self._stop.is_set()
  2565	
  2566	    def run_forever(
  2567	        self,
  2568	        *,
  2569	        install_signals: bool = True,
  2570	        max_ticks: int | None = None,
  2571	    ) -> int:
  2572	        """Steady-state loop: tick, then sleep one interval, until stopped.
  2573	
  2574	        On stop (SIGTERM via the installed handler, ``request_stop()``, or
  2575	        ``max_ticks`` reached) the loop drains cleanly: writes
  2576	        ``shutdown_began`` + ``shutdown_complete`` to the decision log,
  2577	        then returns the number of ticks executed (ADR-0021 §9 L5).
  2578	
  2579	        ``install_signals`` is False-able so tests can drive the loop from
  2580	        a non-main thread (where ``signal.signal`` would raise).
  2581	        ``max_ticks`` bounds the loop for tests; production passes None.
  2582	        """
  2583	        if install_signals:
  2584	            self._install_signal_handlers()
  2585	        # Startup line names the loaded rule registry when the Tier-1 engine
  2586	        # is wired (Deploy AC: registry visible at startup). The engine also
  2587	        # logs its full rule list at construction.
  2588	        rule_names = getattr(self._engine, "rule_names", None)
  2589	        rules_desc = ",".join(rule_names()) if callable(rule_names) else "n/a"
  2590	        logger.info(
  2591	            "[pipeline_coordinator] entering steady state "
  2592	            "(engine=%s mode=%s tick=%.0fs heartbeat=%.0fs "
  2593	            "capacity_tracking=active capacity_path=%s rules=[%s])",
  2594	            self._engine.engine_version,
  2595	            self._mode_selector.select(),
  2596	            self._config.tick_interval_seconds,
  2597	            self._config.heartbeat_interval_seconds,
  2598	            self._config.capacity_path,
  2599	            rules_desc,
  2600	        )
  2601	        ticks = 0
  2602	        try:
  2603	            while not self._stop.is_set():
  2604	                self.run_once()
  2605	                ticks += 1
  2606	                if max_ticks is not None and ticks >= max_ticks:
  2607	                    break
  2608	                if self._stop.is_set():
  2609	                    break
  2610	                # Interruptible: returns True if stop fired while waiting.
  2611	                if self._sleep(self._config.tick_interval_seconds):
  2612	                    break
  2613	        finally:
  2614	            self._drain()
  2615	        return ticks
  2615	        return ticks
  2616	
  2617	    # ── L5 drain on shutdown (ADR-0021 §9 L5) ──
  2618	
  2619	    def _drain(self) -> None:
  2620	        """Write the shutdown markers and exit cleanly.
  2621	
  2622	        The skeleton has no in-flight LLM consultation to finish (§9 L5
  2623	        step 1 is a no-op here); it writes the ``shutdown_began`` /
  2624	        ``shutdown_complete`` pair (step 2) so L6 crash-recovery can tell a
  2625	        clean shutdown from a crash.
  2626	        """
  2627	        drain_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
  2628	        self._decision_log.append(
  2629	            {
  2630	                "ts": self._clock().isoformat(),
  2631	                "event": "shutdown_began",
  2632	                "engine_version": self._engine.engine_version,
  2633	                "decision_id": drain_id,
  2634	                "pid": os.getpid(),
  2635	            }
  2636	        )
  2637	        # (29f: finish in-flight LLM consultation here, max-wait 60s.)
  2638	        self._decision_log.append(
  2639	            {
  2640	                "ts": self._clock().isoformat(),
  2641	                "event": "shutdown_complete",
  2642	                "engine_version": self._engine.engine_version,
  2643	                "decision_id": drain_id,
  2644	                "pid": os.getpid(),
  2645	            }
  2646	        )
  2647	        logger.info("[pipeline_coordinator] drain complete — exiting cleanly")
  2648	
  2649	    def _install_signal_handlers(self) -> None:
  2650	        if self._signals_installed:
  2651	            return
  2652	
  2653	        def _handler(signum: int, _frame: Any) -> None:
  2654	            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] caught signal %d — draining", signum)
  2655	            self.request_stop()
  2656	
  2657	        for sig in (signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGINT):
  2658	            try:
  2659	                signal.signal(sig, _handler)
  2660	            except (ValueError, OSError) as exc:
  2661	                # Non-main thread / unsupported platform — caller drives
  2662	                # stop via request_stop() instead.
  2663	                logger.debug(
  2664	                    "[pipeline_coordinator] could not install handler for %s: %s",
  2665	                    sig, exc,
  2666	                )
  2667	        self._signals_installed = True
  2668

  2719	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
  2720	    """CLI entrypoint. ``--once`` runs a single tick; default runs forever."""
  2721	    import argparse
  2722	
  2723	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
  2724	    parser.add_argument(
  2725	        "--once",
  2726	        action="store_true",
  2727	        help="run a single tick (heartbeat + one no-op decision) and exit "
  2728	             "(skips cold-start recovery — a diagnostic tick, not a boot)",
  2729	    )
  2730	    parser.add_argument(
  2731	        "--no-cold-start",
  2732	        action="store_true",
  2733	        help="enter the steady-state loop directly, skipping the 4-phase "
  2734	             "cold-start recovery (ADR §3.3) — for local debugging only",
  2735	    )
  2736	    parser.add_argument(
  2737	        "--config-dir",
  2738	        type=Path,
  2739	        default=None,
  2740	        help="override the coordinator config dir (default ~/.config/omnisight/coordinator)",
  2741	    )
  2742	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
  2743	
  2744	    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
  2745	    config = CoordinatorConfig.from_env(config_dir=args.config_dir)
  2746	    action_executor = LiveActionExecutor(config).execute if config.acting else None
  2747	    coordinator = build_default_coordinator(
  2748	        config,
  2749	        acting=config.acting,
  2750	        action_executor=action_executor,
  2751	    )
  2752	    if args.once:
  2753	        coordinator.run_once()
  2754	        return 0
  2755	    if args.no_cold_start:
  2756	        coordinator.run_forever()
  2757	        return 0
  2758	    # Production boot: 4-phase cold-start recovery, then steady-state loop.
  2759	    coordinator.run()
  2760	    return 0

     4	parsing logic in ``auto-runner-codex.py`` with JIRA REST-driven
     5	discovery. Used by ``auto-runner-jira.py`` as the dispatch backbone.
     6	
     7	Public API:
     8	- ``fetch_pickable_tickets(agent_class)`` — JQL search per §16, parsed
     9	  into TicketSnapshot objects (compatible with backend.agents.scheduler)
    10	- ``transition_to_in_progress(key, bot_account_id)`` — TODO → In Progress
    11	- ``transition_to_under_review(key, gerrit_url)`` — In Progress → Under Review
    12	- ``transition_back_to_todo(key, reason)`` — In Progress → TODO (revert)
    13	- ``add_comment(key, body)`` — append ADF comment
    14	- ``parse_prerequisites(ticket)`` — extract YAML block from description
    15	
    16	Authentication: reads ``~/.config/omnisight/jira-claude.env`` /
    17	``~/.config/omnisight/jira-codex.env`` etc. based on agent_class.
    18	"""
    19	from __future__ import annotations

    69	# ADR-0033 §6 / S12.G v2 spec §3.6: runners (L3) MUST refuse pickup of any
    70	# ticket carrying a ``class:operator-window-*`` or ``class:operator-rehearsal``
    71	# label, even when the same ticket also carries ``class:subscription-*``.
    72	# Refusal is silent — no JIRA comment, only a structured audit log line — so
    73	# operators (L1/L2) can drive the ticket without runner interference.
    74	REFUSAL_LABEL_PREFIXES = ("class:operator-window-", "class:operator-rehearsal")
    75	
    76	
    77	def _runner_refuses_pickup(labels: list[str]) -> tuple[bool, str | None]:
    78	    """Return ``(True, matching_label)`` if any label triggers L3 refusal.
    79	
    80	    Operator-window-* / operator-rehearsal labels always win over a
   180	    ``claude-bot-3``...). The bot-username is the canonical instance key
   181	    used downstream for SSH key paths, JIRA cred files, backpressure
   182	    state, and idempotency DBs.
   183	    """
   184	    if instance_id is None:
   185	        instance_id = _instance_id_from_env()
   186	    base = _BASE_BOT_BY_CLASS.get(agent_class)
   187	    if base is None:
   188	        raise ValueError(f"unknown agent_class: {agent_class}")
   189	    if instance_id == DEFAULT_INSTANCE_ID:
   190	        return base
   191	    return f"{base}-{instance_id}"
   192	
   193	
   194	def _gerrit_ssh_key_for_bot(bot_username: str) -> Path:
   195	    """Per-bot SSH private key path: ``~/.config/omnisight/gerrit-<bot>-ed25519``."""
   196	    return CRED_DIR / f"gerrit-{bot_username}-ed25519"
   197	
   198	
   199	def _gerrit_auth_for_instance(
   200	    agent_class: str, instance_id: str | None = None
   201	) -> tuple[str, Path]:
   202	    """Return (bot_username, ssh_key_path) for (agent_class, instance_id).
   203	
   204	    For ``instance_id == "default"`` returns the legacy entry from
   205	    ``_GERRIT_AUTH_BY_CLASS`` so existing ssh-key paths are preserved
   206	    bit-for-bit. For non-default instances, derives a per-bot path
   207	    (``gerrit-codex-bot-2-ed25519`` etc.).
   208	    """
   209	    if instance_id is None:
   210	        instance_id = _instance_id_from_env()
   211	    if instance_id == DEFAULT_INSTANCE_ID:
   212	        legacy = _GERRIT_AUTH_BY_CLASS.get(agent_class)
   213	        if legacy is not None:
   214	            return legacy
   215	    bot_username = resolve_bot_username(agent_class, instance_id)
   216	    return bot_username, _gerrit_ssh_key_for_bot(bot_username)
   217	
   218	
   219	def _cred_paths(agent_class: str, instance_id: str | None = None) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
   220	    """Return (env_file, token_file) for (agent_class, instance_id).
   221	
   222	    Default instance keeps legacy filenames (``jira-codex.env`` /
   223	    ``jira-claude.env``) so existing single-instance setups continue to
   224	    work without re-provisioning. Non-default instances use bot-keyed
   225	    filenames (``jira-codex-bot-2.env``, ``jira-claude-bot-3.env`` ...).
   226	    """
   227	    if instance_id is None:
   228	        instance_id = _instance_id_from_env()
   229	    if instance_id == DEFAULT_INSTANCE_ID:
   230	        if agent_class in ("subscription-codex", "api-openai"):
   231	            return CRED_DIR / "jira-codex.env", CRED_DIR / "jira-codex-token"
   232	        return CRED_DIR / "jira-claude.env", CRED_DIR / "jira-claude-token"
   233	    bot_username = resolve_bot_username(agent_class, instance_id)
   234	    return CRED_DIR / f"jira-{bot_username}.env", CRED_DIR / f"jira-{bot_username}-token"
   235	
   236	
   237	def _load_env(env_file: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
   238	    out: dict[str, str] = {}
   239	    for line in env_file.read_text().splitlines():
   240	        line = line.strip()
   241	        if not line or line.startswith("#") or "=" not in line:
   242	            continue
   243	        k, _, v = line.partition("=")
   244	        out[k.strip()] = v.strip()
   245	    return out
   246	
   247

  3291	            live_foreign = label

   160	    Empty string is treated as unset to make ``unset OMNISIGHT_RUNNER_INSTANCE_ID``

   167	_BASE_BOT_BY_CLASS = {
   168	    "subscription-codex": "codex-bot",
   169	    "api-openai": "codex-bot",
   170	    "subscription-claude": "claude-bot",
   171	    "api-anthropic": "claude-bot",
   172	}
   173	
   174	
   175	def resolve_bot_username(agent_class: str, instance_id: str | None = None) -> str:
   176	    """Map (agent_class, instance_id) → Gerrit/JIRA bot username.
   177	
   178	    Default instance returns the legacy bare names (``codex-bot`` /
   179	    ``claude-bot``). Non-default instances append the ID (``codex-bot-2``,
   180	    ``claude-bot-3``...). The bot-username is the canonical instance key
   181	    used downstream for SSH key paths, JIRA cred files, backpressure
   182	    state, and idempotency DBs.
   183	    """
   184	    if instance_id is None:
   185	        instance_id = _instance_id_from_env()
   186	    base = _BASE_BOT_BY_CLASS.get(agent_class)
   187	    if base is None:
   188	        raise ValueError(f"unknown agent_class: {agent_class}")
   189	    if instance_id == DEFAULT_INSTANCE_ID:
   190	        return base
   248	@dataclass(frozen=True)
   249	class DispatchClient:
   250	    """Authenticated JIRA REST client scoped to one agent_class."""
   251	
   252	    agent_class: str
   253	    base_url: str
   254	    project_key: str
   255	    auth_header: str
   256	    bot_account_id: str
   257	    bot_email: str
   258	
   259	
   260	def make_client(agent_class: str, instance_id: str | None = None) -> DispatchClient:
   261	    if instance_id is None:
   262	        instance_id = _instance_id_from_env()
   263	    env_file, token_file = _cred_paths(agent_class, instance_id)
   264	    env = _load_env(env_file)
   265	    token = token_file.read_text().strip()
   266	    # Email key resolution: prefer the instance-agnostic ``OMNISIGHT_JIRA_BOT_EMAIL``
   267	    # (recommended for new per-instance .env files), then fall back to the legacy
   268	    # class-specific keys so existing default-instance .env files keep working.
   269	    legacy_key = "OMNISIGHT_JIRA_CLAUDE_EMAIL" if "claude" in env_file.name else "OMNISIGHT_JIRA_CODEX_EMAIL"
   270	    email = env.get("OMNISIGHT_JIRA_BOT_EMAIL") or env.get(legacy_key)
   271	    if not email:
   272	        raise RuntimeError(
   273	            f"{env_file} has no email key set; expected OMNISIGHT_JIRA_BOT_EMAIL "
   274	            f"or {legacy_key}."
   275	        )
   275	        )
   276	    raw = f"{email}:{token}".encode()
   277	    auth = "Basic " + b64encode(raw).decode()
   278	    site = env["OMNISIGHT_JIRA_SITE_URL"].rstrip("/")
   279	    project = env.get("OMNISIGHT_JIRA_PROJECT_KEY", "OP")
   280	    # accountId via /myself
   281	    me = _request_raw("GET", site + "/rest/api/3/myself", auth, None)
   282	    return DispatchClient(
   283	        agent_class=agent_class,
   284	        base_url=site + "/rest/api/3",
   285	        project_key=project,
   286	        auth_header=auth,
   287	        bot_account_id=me["accountId"],
   288	        bot_email=email,
   289	    )
   290	
   291	
   292	def _request_raw(
   293	    method: str,
   294	    url: str,
   295	    auth_header: str,
   296	    body: dict | None,
   297	    idem_key: str | None = None,
   298	) -> dict:
   299	    data = json.dumps(body).encode() if body is not None else None
   300	    headers = {
   301	        "Authorization": auth_header,
   302	        "Content-Type": "application/json",
   303	        "Accept": "application/json",
   304	    }
   305	    if idem_key:
   306	        headers["X-Atlassian-Token"] = "no-check"
   307	        headers["X-Idempotency-Key"] = idem_key
   308	    req = urllib.request.Request(
   309	        url, data=data, method=method,
   310	        headers=headers,
   311	    )
   312	    try:
   313	        with BREAKERS["jira_rest"].call(urllib.request.urlopen, req, timeout=30) as resp:
   314	            payload = resp.read().decode()
   315	            return json.loads(payload) if payload else {}

**BLOCKER**

1. **Startup-2 over-reach is real and the audit is accurate.**  
   `interrupted_tickets()` selects every `In Progress` / `進行中` issue in the project with only `ORDER BY updated ASC`; it fetches only `status, updated`, so it cannot inspect `coord-skip`, `coord-quarantine`, `tier:X`, assignee, or META fields. Evidence: [/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1186](/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1186), lines 1193-1200.  
   In acting mode, those tickets can be mutated by:
   - `transition_under_review()` for mergeable Gerrit changes: line 2307
   - `mark_resumable()` / add `runner:resume-from-feature-branch`: lines 2314, 1249-1250
   - `reset_to_todo()` / transition back to To Do: lines 2322, 1264-1275
   - `mention_operator()` / JIRA comment plus `needs-operator-action` for high urgency, comment only for medium: lines 2330-2335, 1313-1324

2. **Startup-3 over-reach is real and slightly worse than the table states.**  
   `_build_stale_sweep_plan()` selects any issue with `claim:*`, any issue with `runner-blocked:waiting-*`, or any `To Do` issue with any assignee. It has no guard for `coord-skip`, `coord-quarantine`, `tier:X`, human assignee, or META. Evidence: [/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1602](/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1602), lines 1619-1627.  
   Mutations are:
   - remove each stale `claim:*`: line 2358
   - clear assignee after stale-claim cleanup, regardless of whether the assignee is human: line 2364
   - clear assignee for `To Do AND assignee is not EMPTY` with no claim: line 2371
   - remove resolved `runner-blocked:waiting-*`: line 2380  
   The human-assignee blast radius claim is accurate.

3. **The audit’s L6 inventory is incomplete.**  
   Current shipped code has two L6 paths. The audit mentions only the legacy `shutdown_began` path, but the primary current path replays unmatched `tick_intent` records: [/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:2118](/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:2118), lines 2152-2164. That path calls `_execute_tick_actions()` on replayed actions: lines 2201-2202. In acting boot, `main()` wires `LiveActionExecutor` when `config.acting` is true: lines 2744-2750.  
   Therefore L6 can replay all live action kinds supported by `LiveActionExecutor`, not only relabel/transition/file_ticket:
   - relabel: add/remove labels and clear assignee when removing `claim:*`, lines 642-666
   - transition to To Do or Under Review, lines 591-626
   - mention operator, lines 674-699
   - mark followup, lines 702-723
   - escalate, lines 725-750
   - file_ticket fallback comment + `needs-operator-action`, lines 752-793  
   Important nuance: `LiveActionExecutor` does re-check `coord-skip` and `coord-quarantine` before these live mutations, lines 538-562. It still does not guard `tier:X`, human assignee, or META.

**SHOULD-FIX**

4. **“No cold-start phase honors any operator-intent guard” needs a small revision.**  
   For Startup-2 and Startup-3: true. Their direct gateway mutators do not perform the live action executor’s `coord-skip` / `coord-quarantine` recheck.  
   For L6 tick-intent replay: not fully true, because replay through `LiveActionExecutor` does honor `coord-skip` / `coord-quarantine` at execution time. Evidence: lines 555-561. Legacy L6 gateway calls still do not have that protection.

5. **Steady-state comparison is mostly right but understated.**  
   `coordinator_jql()` excludes only `coord-skip`, not `coord-quarantine`: [/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:974](/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:974), line 979. The live action executor recheck excludes both `coord-skip` and `coord-quarantine`, lines 555-561. The audit should say cold-start Startup-2/3 are weaker than both steady-state polling and live-action execution.

6. **Q4: excluding `tier:X` should not strand legitimate runner work.**  
   Runner pickup JQL explicitly excludes `tier:X`: [/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/agents/jira_dispatch.py:357](/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/agents/jira_dispatch.py:357), line 364. A legitimate runner pickup should therefore not originate from `tier:X`. If a `tier:X` issue has runner residue, that is already an exceptional/manual state and should be handled by operator-visible recovery, not blind cold-start mutation.

**NICE / Audit Corrections**

7. **Startup-1 inventory is substantially correct.**  
   `InfraAuditResult.down` now includes only `expected == "yes"` and not live: [/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1036](/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1036), lines 1041-1043. Acting mutation is `systemctl --user start <unit>`: lines 1167-1174 and 2093. If still down, it posts an infra-level operator comment/label via `mention_operator("OP", ...)`: lines 2104-2112 and 1319-1324.

8. **No live git mutation found in cold-start.**  
   Gerrit and branch checks are read-only probes. `branch_has_commits()` and `gerrit_change_mergeable()` do not write git/JIRA by themselves: lines 1235-1247. Cold-start does write the decision log locally throughout startup, but that is not live JIRA/git/systemctl blast radius.

**Q1-Q6**

Q1: S1+S2 are not sufficient. They fix the known `coord-skip` / `tier:X` examples, but miss human-assigned non-`tier:X` tickets and unassigned/non-`tier:X` META rollups. S3 and S4 are needed for correctness.

Q2: I did not find a canonical all-bot JIRA account allowlist. `DispatchClient` knows only the current client’s `bot_account_id`: [/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/agents/jira_dispatch.py:248](/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/agents/jira_dispatch.py:248), lines 252-258. Bot usernames are derived by class/instance, but JIRA account IDs come from each env’s `/myself`: lines 167-191 and 260-288. Drift risk is real for per-instance bots.

Q3: Use JQL for coarse, always-true exclusions (`coord-skip`, `coord-quarantine`, `tier:X`, META where expressible), plus Python-side defensive filtering before mutation. Human-vs-bot assignee needs Python unless a maintained accountId allowlist is added.

Q4: No, a legitimate runner target should not be `tier:X`; pickup excludes it at JQL level.

Q5: Done-state claim cleanup is probably harmless but not flip-blocking. It is still live write churn over many boots. Excluding terminal statuses from Startup-3 claim cleanup is a reasonable follow-up, but lower priority than operator-intent guards.

Q6: The audit missed L6 tick-intent replay as a broader mutation path. It also should distinguish L6’s live executor recheck from Startup-2/3’s unguarded gateway mutators.

**Recommended Minimal-Correct Fix Scope**

Implement S1, S2, S3, and S4. Put coarse filters in Startup-2 and Startup-3 JQL, fetch enough fields to prove labels/assignee/META state, and add Python-side last-mile guards before direct cold-start gateway mutation. Include `coord-quarantine` with `coord-skip`.

Verdict: **audit-needs-revision**. Core blocker is sound and severe, but the L6 inventory and “no cold-start phase honors any operator-intent guard” claim need correction.
